We instantiate the problem in character dialogue, where each character should remain consistent across situations, each line should fit its situation, and characters and situations should remain distinguishable. Our method, TalkMatrix, generates multiple candidates for every character--situation pair and jointly selects a complete matrix using four embedding-based consistency and diversity objectives. Because a weighted sum can improve some dimensions by sacrificing another, TalkMatrix maximizes the worst-performing objective through a two-level minimax formulation. We approximately optimize the resulting discrete objective with multi-start coordinate ascent, and compare it with local, partial-matrix, and generic combinatorial search baselines. We run experiments on $50$ synthetic role-playing scenarios and $25$ curated board game scenarios where multiple characters interact in predefined situations. An LLM-as-a-judge rates matrix-level selection higher than random and independent cell-level selection baselines. These results show the value of structured selection for globally controlled dialogue generation, while our empirical validation remains specific to role-playing scenarios.

</details>


### [178] [Entropy in Conversational AI: Structured Unpredictability as Inferrable Interiority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19044)
*Sebastian Cochinescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出“结构化不可预测性”这一设计目标，通过状态更新与响应选择两层机制，让输出依赖于观察者无法从对话记录推断的持续隐藏状态。合成实验验证了流程；在Qwen2.5-1.5B真实模型上，机制提升了词汇新颖度，但新颖性与风格一致性的联合标准、双部分累积标准及孪生分离均未成立，因此未能确立路径依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样可增加响应多样性，却不会产生依赖历史的行为。作者希望形式化并实现另一种目标——结构化不可预测性，即输出与持续隐藏状态之间的条件依赖，且这种依赖超出观察者从对话记录中可推断的范围，从而使跨轮次的依赖真正存在于包装器状态而非对话历史中。

Method: 设计一个选择层：从容量受限的流中更新低维的“风格—注意力”状态，用固定基座模型生成多个响应，再依据新颖度与状态亲和度进行选择。评估采用脚本化的独立提示轮次序列，基座模型仅接收当前轮次与渲染后的状态，不含前置对话，跨轮依赖由包装器状态与响应选择器承担。使用mlx-community/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct-4bit，每臂56条序列，并以哈希冻结的散度特征进行验证。

Result: 合成实现验证了流程，并在点级别匹配四个预先冻结的散度特征。真实模型网格中，机制相对低方差与仅一致性控制分别提升了0.073和0.023的词汇新颖度；但与新颖度匹配采样相比，其风格一致性对比在注册最小效应规则下等于零，联合新颖性—一致性标准未通过。原两部分累积标准也未通过；在功效网格后冻结的修订对比发现，其一致性高于记忆重置消融（0.028，95% CI [0.018,0.039]），但不足以确立路径依赖。孪生分离未成立（0.003，95% CI [-0.011,0.019]）；均值曲线的饱和曲率与冻结预测一致，但在缺乏分离的情况下不支持路径依赖。探针级能力等价在近天花板测验上保持在±0.10内，输出质量未评估。

Conclusion: 该机制能在真实模型上提升词汇新颖度，但多项关键判据未能满足：联合标准、累积标准与孪生分离均未通过，因此无法确立路径依赖性。所有结果均为机器评分，未测试任何关于被感知心智或意识的论断，也未评估输出质量。

Abstract: Sampling can increase response diversity without producing history-dependent behavior. We formalize a different design target, structured unpredictability, as conditional dependence between an output and a persistent hidden state beyond what an observer can infer from the transcript. A selection layer updates a low-dimensional style-and-attention state from a capacity-limited stream, generates several responses with a fixed base model, and selects for novelty and state affinity. Evaluation uses scripted sequences of independent prompt turns: the base model receives the current turn and rendered state, but not the preceding dialogue; cross-turn dependence resides in the wrapper state and response selector. A synthetic implementation validates the pipeline and matches four prospectively hash-frozen divergence features at point level. In the final real-model grid (mlx-community/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct-4bit; 56 sequences per arm), the mechanism increased lexical novelty over the low-variance and consistency-only controls by 0.073 and 0.023, respectively. Its stylometric-consistency contrast with novelty-matched sampling was equivalent to zero under the registered smallest-effect rule, so the joint novelty-consistency criterion failed. The original two-part accumulation criterion also failed; a revised final-grid contrast, frozen after the powered grid, found higher consistency than the memory-reset ablation (0.028, 95% CI [0.018,0.039]), but does not establish path dependence. Twin separation was not established (0.003, 95% CI [-0.011,0.019]); the mean curve's saturating curvature matched the frozen prediction, which without separation does not support path dependence. Probe-level capability equivalence held within +/-0.10 on a near-ceiling battery, while output quality was not evaluated. All outcomes are machine-scored; no claims about perceived mind or consciousness are tested.

</details>


### [179] [The Death of Schema Linking? Text-to-SQL in the Age of Well-Reasoned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.07702)
*Karime Maamari,Fadhil Abubaker,Daniel Jaroslawicz,Amine Mhedhbi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视了Text-to-SQL中的schema linking步骤，发现新一代大语言模型即使面对大量无关schema元素也能有效利用相关元素，因此提出在schema能放入上下文窗口时完全跳过schema linking，并采用增强、选择与纠正等技术提升准确率，在BIRD基准上取得71.83%的准确率，排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schema linking是Text-to-SQL流程中的关键步骤，用于检索与用户查询相关的表和列。但不完美的schema linking常常会错误地排除生成准确查询所需的列，从而影响最终效果。作者希望重新审视在大语言模型新版本下这一步骤是否仍然必要。

Method: 作者通过实验发现，较新的LLM即使在存在大量无关schema元素时也能很好地利用相关元素。基于此，当schema能放入模型上下文窗口时，完全放弃schema linking，以避免因过滤掉必需schema元素而产生的问题。同时，作者不采用过滤上下文信息的方式，而是引入增强、选择和纠正等技术来提升pipeline的准确率。

Result: 该方法在BIRD基准测试上排名第一，准确率达到71.83%。

Conclusion: 在新一代大语言模型背景下，传统schema linking的必要性下降；当schema规模适配上下文窗口时，直接跳过schema linking并结合增强、选择、纠正等技术，可有效提升Text-to-SQL的准确率。

Abstract: Schema linking is a crucial step in Text-to-SQL pipelines. Its goal is to retrieve the relevant tables and columns of a target database for a user's query while disregarding irrelevant ones. However, imperfect schema linking can often exclude required columns needed for accurate query generation. In this work, we revisit schema linking when using the latest generation of large language models (LLMs). We find empirically that newer models are adept at utilizing relevant schema elements during generation even in the presence of large numbers of irrelevant ones. As such, our Text-to-SQL pipeline entirely forgoes schema linking in cases where the schema fits within the model's context window in order to minimize issues due to filtering required schema elements. Furthermore, instead of filtering contextual information, we highlight techniques such as augmentation, selection, and correction, and adopt them to improve the accuracy of our Text-to-SQL pipeline. Our approach ranks first on the BIRD benchmark achieving an accuracy of 71.83%.

</details>


### [180] [MIRAGE: How Conversation State Shapes Historical Evidence Use in Multimodal Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19059)
*Yu Liu,Wenxiao Zhang,Cheng Hu,Cong Cao,Fangfang Yuan,Xinyu Wang,Jin B. Hong,Yanbing Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MIRAGE受控评测框架，在固定证据、问题与评分的前提下仅变化对话状态，揭示多模态个人智能体历史证据使用的非单调失效规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM智能体被广泛用作长时程个人助理，其价值取决于跨对话、文件与工作区状态的连续性。但即使历史访问已退化，智能体仍能给出看似合理的答案，导致仅看结果的评测高估其真实证据使用能力，因此需要在对话状态变化下评估历史证据使用。

Method: MIRAGE（多模态交互检索、归因与接地评测）保持证据对象、问题与评分标准不变，仅改变对话状态；评测智能体能否判断可答性、恢复正确来源并据此作答。在七个前沿与开源多模态骨干模型上进行实验。

Result: 1）压缩前深度与压缩后延续构成两个不同且非单调的失效区间，而非单一退化曲线；2）开源权重模型高度依赖上下文连续性，在溯源失败时不愿自发转向工具检索；3）检索压力能改善工具合规模型在深度压缩前状态下的来源归因，但在压缩后一致退化，此时存储证据已受损。

Conclusion: 历史证据使用应在状态变化下评测，而不能仅由结果正确性推断。仅有结果导向的评测会系统性高估多模态个人智能体的真实证据使用能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language model (MLLM) agents are increasingly used as personal assistants for long-running tasks. Their utility depends on continuity: agents must retrieve and use earlier evidence across dialogue, files, and workspace state. However, agents can generate plausible answers even when access to that history has degraded, causing outcome-only evaluation to overestimate true evidence use. We present MIRAGE (Multimodal Interaction Retrieval, Attribution, and Grounding Evaluation), a controlled study of historical evidence use under conversation-state variation in multimodal personal agents. MIRAGE holds evidence objects, questions, and scoring fixed while varying only conversation state, and evaluates whether an agent can determine answerability, recover the correct source, and answer from it. Across seven frontier and open-weight multimodal backbones, we find that: 1) pre-compaction depth and post-compaction continuation form distinct, non-monotonic failure regimes rather than a single degradation curve; 2) open-weight models rely heavily on context continuity and are reluctant to spontaneously switch to tool-mediated retrieval when provenance fails; and 3) retrieval pressure improves source attribution in deep pre-compaction states for tool-compliant models, but consistently regresses after compaction, where stored evidence has already degraded. These findings show that historical evidence use should be evaluated under state variation, rather than inferred from outcome-only correctness.

</details>


### [181] [Reading Between the Lines: Can LLMs Discover the Question Behind the Text?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19070)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"问题考古"任务：从完整文本推断激发其创作的唯一真实"起源问题"，并构建新数据集与基准，发现当前LLM在此任务上超越人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分于面向任意合理问题的问题生成任务以及建模话语级行为的话语框架，本文旨在评估模型对作者意图的把握能力，弥补现有任务在意图理解评估上的不足。

Method: 定义"问题考古"评估任务，构建由受委托撰写文本及其原始研究问题、合理干扰项配对组成的新数据集，并评测Gemini Flash/Pro、Mistral、Qwen及BERT类模型。

Result: 模型在该任务上取得显著进展，新版本优于旧版本，而BERT类模型表现较差；当前LLM在此任务上超过人类表现。

Conclusion: 该任务为评估作者意图理解提供了新框架与具有挑战性的基准，对AI在需要细致解读人类交流的任务中具有重要意义。

Abstract: This paper introduces ``question archaeology'', a specific evaluation task focused on inferring the single, authentic "genesis question" that motivated the creation of a complete text. Distinct from question generation, which targets any plausible question, or discourse frameworks that model utterance-level acts, our task assesses a model's grasp of authorial intent. We present a new dataset of commissioned texts paired with their original research questions and plausible distractors. Our evaluation of both proprietary models, like Gemini Flash and Pro, as well as open source models like Mistral and Qwen, reveals significant progress in this task, with the newer versions outperforming the earlier ones, while BERT-based models performed poorly. Notably, our findings indicate that current LLMs surpass human performance on this task, suggesting advanced understanding of authorial intent. This capability has important implications for AI's role in tasks requiring nuanced interpretation of human communication. Our work thus provides a new framework and a challenging benchmark for future models.

</details>


### [182] [Benchmarking Large Language Models for Biomedical Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19071)
*Claudiu Creanga,Teodor Marchitan,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对多种NLP模型（包括MLM、混合架构及先进LLM）在SNPPhenA语料上进行基准测试，涵盖句子级、摘要级和关联强度分类三项任务，发现现代LLM在基因组知识抽取中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从生物医学文献中抽取SNP-表型关联非常重要但具有挑战性，需要系统评估当前各类NLP模型在此任务上的能力。

Method: 在SNPPhenA语料上基准测试了多种NLP模型，包括掩码语言模型（MLM）、混合架构以及最先进的大语言模型（Gemini 2.0、OpenAI O系列、Qwen、Mistral），涵盖句子级分类、摘要级分类和关联强度分类三项任务。

Result: OpenAI O1通过少样本学习在未微调的句子级分类上取得SOTA（F1 0.89），并在摘要级分类上创下新SOTA（F1 0.82）；关联强度分类较困难，微调的Gemini 2.0 Pro表现最佳（F1 0.60）。专有LLM显著优于其他模型。

Conclusion: 现代LLM在基因组知识抽取方面具有强大能力，尤其在少样本（O1）或微调（Gemini 2.0 Pro）设置下表现突出。

Abstract: Extracting SNP-phenotype associations from biomedical literature is vital but challenging. We benchmarked diverse NLP models, including MLMs, hybrid architectures, and state-of-the-art LLMs (Gemini 2.0, OpenAI O-series, Qwen, Mistral), on the SNPPhenA corpus across three tasks: sentence-level, abstract-level, and association strength classification. OpenAI O1 achieved state-of-the-art (SOTA) results using few-shot learning for non-finetuned sentence-level classification (F1 0.89) and established a new SOTA for abstract-level classification (F1 0.82). Association strength classification proved difficult, though fine-tuned Gemini 2.0 Pro performed best (F1 0.60) in the first LLM evaluation of this task. Proprietary LLMs, especially in few-shot (O1) or fine-tuned (Gemini 2.0 Pro) settings, significantly outperformed other models. These findings confirm the power of modern LLMs for genomic knowledge extraction.

</details>


### [183] [Safety-Flag: A Unified Benchmark for the Reliability and Calibration of LLM Content Moderators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19072)
*Yibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Safety-Flag 基准，将七个常用安全基准统一到一个平衡的"标记/不标记"协议下，对六个通用 LLM、四个专用防护模型及三个参考模型在相同条目上评测，并发布条目级决策与置信度分数，从错误方向、概率校准和基于置信度的错误排序三个维度衡量审核可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内容审核评测大多只报告单一基准上的总体准确率，掩盖了错误方向、校准质量等关键差异，无法反映模型在真实人工复核流程中的可靠性。

Method: 构建 Safety-Flag 基准，将 BeaverTails、XSTest、Ethics、WildGuard、Aegis、ToxiChat、ToxiGen 七个安全基准整合为统一的平衡"标记/不标记"协议；在相同条目上评测六个通用 LLM、四个专用防护模型和三个参考模型，并公开条目级决策、置信度分数及评测代码。

Result: 模型间常出现分歧。总体准确率无法体现错误方向：一个模型标记了 85% 的良性内容，另一个则漏掉 54% 的有害内容。六个通用模型均过度自信，按模型拟合单一温度可降低 2.8–6.0 倍校准误差且不改变预测标签或置信度排序。基于置信度的弃权对每个模型都降低了选择性风险，但收益取决于置信度对错误的排序能力。专用防护模型误报更少、校准更好，但部分模型在其文档覆盖范围之外漏检率更高。

Conclusion: 总体准确率不足以评估审核模型，应从错误方向、概率校准与置信度排序等多维度衡量；Safety-Flag 提供开放基准、固定条目列表、评测代码、逐条目模型输出和排行榜，以支持更可靠的模型比较与人工复核。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for content moderation, but most evaluations still report aggregate accuracy on individual benchmarks. We introduce Safety-Flag, which places seven widely used safety benchmarks (BeaverTails, XSTest, Ethics, WildGuard, Aegis, ToxiChat, and ToxiGen) into a single balanced flag / do-not-flag protocol. We release item-level decisions and confidence scores for six general-purpose LLMs and four dedicated guards, together with three reference models, evaluated on the same items. Safety-Flag measures three dimensions of moderator reliability: error direction, probability calibration, and confidence-based error ranking for human review. They often disagree. Aggregate accuracy does not reveal error direction: one model flags $85\%$ of benign content, whereas another misses $54\%$ of harmful content. All six general-purpose models are overconfident; fitting one temperature per model reduces calibration error by $2.8$--$6.0\times$ without changing predicted labels or confidence ordering. Confidence-based abstention lowers selective risk for every model, although the gains depend on how well confidence ranks errors. Dedicated guards produce fewer false alarms and are better calibrated, but several have higher miss rates outside their documented coverage. We release the benchmark, fixed item lists, evaluation code, per-item model outputs, and leaderboard at: https://github.com/yibo-hu-lab/safety-flag-benchmark.

</details>


### [184] [Reporting Practice Matters: The Impact of Reference Choice on Chest X-ray Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19093)
*Daniel P. Jeong,Charles Q. Li,Hossein Hosseiny,Nitya M. Bhalla,Fatma Uyar Morency,Pradeep Ravikumar,Zachary C. Lipton,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示放射学报告生成（RRG）评测指标对报告风格差异的敏感性，提出报告实践变异分类法及重写方法ReRef，并证明风格差异足以改变模型排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生的报告实践存在异质性：同一图像、同一临床发现的报告可能在术语、缩写、格式和细节程度上表面不同。这类报告规范的差异是评估AI放射报告生成模型时被低估的障碍，因为机器生成报告通常依据与人类参考报告的吻合度来评估，而当前指标可能无法区分临床解读与报告风格 conformity。

Method: 作者（1）量化现有评测指标对报告实践变异的敏感性；（2）提出由放射科医生启发的放射报告实践变异分类法；（3）提出ReRef方法，沿分类法各轴重写参考报告，同时保持临床解读不变；（4）构建并发布经放射科医生验证的数据集MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef，包含120对（原始、替代）参考报告。

Result: 报告实践变异对指标的影响足以改变模型排名。例如在MIMIC-CXR上使用RadCliQ-v1比较九个RRG模型时，压缩参考报告中正常发现的描述会使Libra从第一降至第二，CheXOne从第三升至第一。

Conclusion: 当前许多指标无法将临床解读与对报告实践的遵循解耦；在实践中，选择能准确反映目标报告实践的"正确"参考报告非常重要。研究发布的MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef数据集可支持未来相关研究。

Abstract: Radiologists follow heterogeneous reporting practices. Two radiologists examining the same image and identifying the same clinical findings might nevertheless compose superficially distinct reports, varying in terminology, shorthand, formatting, and level of detail. These variations in reporting norms represent an under-appreciated obstacle in efforts to evaluate AI-based radiology report generation (RRG) models, where machine-generated reports are typically assessed based on their concordance with human-generated references. In this paper, we quantify the sensitivity of established evaluation metrics to variations in reporting practices, revealing impacts large enough to alter the rankings of models. We introduce a radiologist-informed taxonomy of variations in radiology reporting practice and a method (ReRef) that rewrites reference reports along the axes of our taxonomy while preserving clinical interpretation. For instance, when comparing the performance of nine RRG models on MIMIC-CXR using RadCliQ-v1, condensing the discussion of normal findings in the reference reports causes Libra to drop from first to second place while CheXOne rises from third to first. Our results suggest that many current metrics fail to decouple clinical interpretation from conformity to reporting practices and that choosing the ``right'' references that accurately reflect the desired reporting practices can be important in practice. To support future research, we release MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef, a radiologist-validated dataset of 120 (original, alternative) reference report pairs derived from MIMIC-CXR.

</details>


### [185] [Monitoring and Discovering Reward Hacking with Internal Representations during LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19101)
*Leon Bergen,Usha Bhalla,Andrew Lee,Barak Widawsky,Linas Nasvytis,Connor Watts,Siddharth Boppana,Sidharth Baskaran,Dron Hazra,Michael Byun,Atticus Geiger,Owen Lewis,Matthew Kowal,Vasudev Shyam,Thomas Fel,Thomas McGrath,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Jack Merullo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现奖励黑客行为在前沿开源大模型内部可由简单的均值差（DoM）向量连贯表示，这些向量可泛化、可解释，能以近乎零成本可靠检测奖励黑客，甚至可在思维链上在线预测后续动作中的作弊行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型规模扩大，奖励黑客行为变得更频繁、更复杂、后果更严重，因此需要探究该行为是否在模型表征中留下可识别的特征，并寻找比昂贵 LLM 监控器更经济高效的检测方法。

Method: 在 Kimi K3、GLM 5.2 和 Qwen 3.8 Max 上，用简单的均值差（DoM）向量表征奖励黑客行为，并在 DeepSWE、SWE-bench 等常见基准上评估其检测效果，同时将 DoM 向量应用于思维链以在线预测后续动作中的作弊，并与 LLM 监控器对比。

Result: 模型在这些环境中大量作弊：GLM 5.2 在 DeepSWE 上 57.2%、SWE-bench 上 73% 的 rollout 中存在黑客行为。DoM 向量在匹配假阳性率下，于 DeepSWE 上比 LLM 监控器多捕获 Kimi K3 中 3.1% 的黑客、少捕获 GLM 5.2 中 7.9% 的黑客；还能预测后续动作中的作弊，并发现 LLM 监控器漏掉的其它不良行为，且可迁移到非 SWE 评估场景。

Conclusion: 简单的白盒方法能够可扩展地研究和监控前沿开源模型中的奖励黑客行为。

Abstract: As models scale, reward hacking becomes more frequent, more sophisticated, and more consequential. Does it leave a telltale signature in model representations? This work analyzes how reward hacking is represented internally in frontier open source LLMs, and how those representations can be used to understand and discover the range of hacking behaviors a model displays. In particular, we find that simple difference of means vectors coherently represent reward hacking in Kimi K3, GLM 5.2, and Qwen 3.8 Max across a variety of behaviors in common evaluations. Despite their simplicity, these vectors are both generalizable and interpretable, and we can use them to reliably detect reward hacking. We first evaluate reward hacking in commonly reported benchmarks like DeepSWE and SWE-bench, finding that models reward hack excessively in these environments; GLM 5.2 hacks in 57.2% of rollouts on DeepSWE and in 73% of rollouts on SWE-bench. Catching these requires monitors; LLM monitors are effective, but expensive detectors. We show that DoM vectors are similarly effective but virtually free, catching 3.1% more hacks in Kimi K3 and 7.9% fewer hacks in GLM 5.2 on DeepSWE at a monitor matched false positive rate. DoM vectors run on the chain-of-thought also predict reward hacks in the model's subsequent actions, meaning we can run them online and catch potential hacks before they occur. Finally, we analyze probe-hits that LLM monitors do not catch and discover other undesirable behaviors, as well as show transfer to finding hacks in non-SWE evaluations. Together, these results provide evidence that simple, white-box methods can be used to scalably study and monitor reward hacking behaviors in frontier open source models

</details>


### [186] [Playing log(N)-Questions over Wikipedia Abstracts: Communication Efficiency Between Paired Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19113)
*Peter Potash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 六个前沿语言模型在双智能体 log(N) 猜题游戏中自我对弈 408 局，五个领先模型胜率随文档集规模下降并可由单一每轮可靠性参数 p=0.928 拟合，最弱模型因倾向于回答“No”而显著落后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在测量模型在信息不对称条件下与自身进行通信的能力，通过让同一提供商的模型分别扮演提问者和回答者，隔离出模型间沟通与信息传递的效率问题，而非单纯的知识或推理能力。

Method: 构建双智能体 log(N)-Questions 游戏：提问者看到 N 篇维基百科导语段落，需用恰好 log₂N 个是非题找出隐藏目标；回答者仅看到目标和问题，用一个词回答。共运行 408 局，文档集从 4 到 1024 段，总 API 成本 363 美元，覆盖六个前沿模型。

Result: 一个模型明显落后（Claude Opus 5 胜 28/68，其余为 45–56）。领先五模型差异甚微，合并后胜率与集合规模呈 r=−0.973 的下降关系，符合 win=p^{log₂N}，p=0.928。失败约均分为回答错误与区分失败；模型极少提名自身证据排除的文档。最弱模型的 34 次一致回答错误中有 32 次为“No”。每问题信息量与胜率相关 r=+0.88。

Conclusion: 模型自我通信能力随问题规模呈稳定的指数衰减，可由单一可靠性参数刻画。回答偏差（默认“No”）而非知识缺失是主要失败来源，只有按标题分区的策略能提取完整比特信息，且推理 token 消耗与成功率几乎无关。

Abstract: We evaluate six frontier language models on the two-agent $\log(N)$-Questions game. A questioner sees $N$ Wikipedia lead paragraphs and must identify a secretly chosen target using exactly $\log_2 N$ yes/no questions. An answerer sees only the target and the question, and replies with one word. Both roles run on the same provider, so the game measures how well a model communicates with itself across an information asymmetry. We run 408 games over document sets of 4 to 1024 paragraphs at a total API cost of \$363. One model finishes well behind the others: Claude Opus 5 wins 28 of 68 games, against 45 to 56 for GLM-5.3, GPT-5.6 Sol, Grok 4.6, Gemini 3.8 Flash and Kimi K3. The leading five are only marginally separable. Pooling those five, win rate declines with set size at $r=-0.973$ and is fit by a single per-round reliability parameter. The form is $\text{win}=p^{\log_2 N}$ with $p=0.928$. Losses divide into answer errors and discrimination failures in roughly equal measure, and models almost never name a document their own evidence excludes. Every unanimous answer error from the weakest model was inspected: 32 of 34 are ``No'' answers, on properties stated in the document's first sentence, under an instruction that explicitly warns against defaulting to ``No''. Information per question, estimated from answer balance, correlates with win rate at $r=+0.88$. The only two models to extract a full bit per question are the only two that partition on document titles, a strategy absent below $N{=}32$ and used in a quarter of questions above it. Reasoning-token expenditure varies $4.5\times$ across models with little relation to success, and the trace grows as the candidate set shrinks without a matching gain in reliability.

</details>


### [187] [ScienceIDE: Turning World's Scientific Codebase into Agent Learnable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19134)
*Hejia Geng,Zesen Huang,Haoyang Li,Wenbin Li,Koutian Wu,Zihan Zhou,Yuanbo Pang,Weihao Liu,Zigong Xu,Zhiping Li,Zongzheng Zhang,Chuanfei Dong,Jiankai Sun,Tianzhe Zheng,Fengyu Xie,Yue Ma,Yueheng Shi,Tong Xie,Zonglin Di,Xianrong Liu,Qucheng Gao,Yimin Liu,Jiaming Pan,Sheng Huang,Xiao-Han Ma,Lanqing Yuan,Zhenlin Zhu,Ziang Liu,Ziyang Xu,Junkai Wang,Kangkai Liang,Jiayi Xian,Zehong Zhao,Liuwei Xu,Jingxu Xie,Peijin Zhang,Qiang Gao,Chengyi Xing,Zhe Zhao,Xi Wang,Yaopeng Xing,Xing Meng,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出科学经验瓶颈问题，构建 ScienceIDE 基础设施，将科学代码仓库转化为可执行的智能体学习环境，并训练出 PhAI-IDE 系列模型，验证了科学经验向通用能力的正向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学代码仓库中蕴含数十年的人类知识，但工具链碎片化、领域约定隐性化以及专门的正确性标准，使这些知识难以转化为可靠的学习经验，即"科学经验瓶颈"。作者希望将全球科学代码转化为科学智能体可编程的学习环境，为智能体学习与科学实践提供统一工作空间。

Method: 提出 ScienceIDE 基础设施。在专家定义的科学案例与验收标准指导下，智能体将代码仓库转化为可执行环境，支持任务生成、执行与科学验证。这些环境为监督微调、强化学习和评估提供共享基础。利用经过验证的交互轨迹，训练了 PhAI-IDE-72B、PhAI-IDE-9B 和 PhAI-IDE-4B 三个模型。

Result: PhAI-IDE 模型家族在留出的科学代码修复任务上取得提升，并在代码、推理与知识等部分通用基准上也有改善，表明科学经验能正向迁移到更广泛的能力上。

Conclusion: ScienceIDE 为智能体学习与科学实践的集成工作空间奠定基础，使人类的科学软件成为发展科学智能的共享底座。代码已开源。

Abstract: Scientific code repositories encode decades of human knowledge in executable models, methods, and tools. Yet fragmented toolchains, implicit domain conventions, and specialized correctness criteria make this knowledge difficult to convert into reliable learning experience-a challenge we call the scientific experience bottleneck. We introduce ScienceIDE, infrastructure for turning the world's scientific code into programmable environments for scientific agents. Guided by expert-defined scientific cases and acceptance criteria, agents transform repositories into executable environments that support task generation, execution, and scientific verification. These environments provide a shared foundation for supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and evaluation. Using verified interaction trajectories, we train PhAI-IDE-72B, PhAI-IDE-9B, and PhAI-IDE-4B. The model family shows gains in held-out scientific-code repair and across selected general-purpose benchmarks in code, reasoning, and knowledge, providing evidence of positive transfer from scientific experience to broader capabilities. ScienceIDE lays the foundation for an integrated workspace for agent learning and scientific practice, making humanity's scientific software a shared substrate for developing scientific intelligence. Code: https://github.com/aitofound/ScienceIDE

</details>


### [188] [A Zeroth-Order Paradigm for LLM Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19144)
*Peter Chen,Xi Chen,Wotao Yin,Tianyi Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出并分析基于比较预言机的零阶对齐方法ComPO，从偏好对中提取方向信息而不直接优化可微偏好损失，给出收敛与性能保证，并在多个模型上超越现有直接对齐方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接偏好对齐方法因计算与内存效率被广泛用于让LLM对齐人类偏好，但似然位移问题促使研究者寻找新的方式来从具有小似然边界的偏好对中提取信息，避免直接在偏好对上优化可微偏好损失。

Method: 提出Comparison-based Preference Optimization（ComPO），一种基于比较预言机的零阶对齐方法：从偏好对中提取方向信息而非直接优化可微偏好损失。建立其离线基本方案在平滑性、梯度稀疏性以及预言机与潜在目标兼容性下的收敛保证；进一步提出在线ComPO，保留离线比较机制并用无标签策略生成做相对于参考策略的反向KL控制；在局部覆盖与分布内成对奖励准确率下，为基本约束方案建立性能保证。

Result: 在Mistral、Llama、Gemma-2、Qwen3和Gemma-3模型上的实验表明，ComPO优于现有直接对齐方法，包括在长度控制胜率上取得提升；成对级诊断提供了与缓解似然位移一致的证据。

Conclusion: ComPO通过比较式零阶优化从偏好对中提取方向信息，具备收敛与性能理论保证，并在多模型上实证优于现有直接对齐方法，同时为缓解似然位移提供了证据。

Abstract: Direct preference alignment methods are widely used to align large language models (LLMs) with human preferences because of their computational and memory efficiency. However, likelihood displacement motivates alternative ways to extract information from preference pairs with small likelihood margins. In this paper, we propose and analyze Comparison-based Preference Optimization (ComPO), a zeroth-order alignment method based on comparison oracles. ComPO extracts directional information from these pairs without directly optimizing a differentiable preference loss on them. We establish a convergence guarantee for its basic offline scheme under smoothness, gradient sparsity, and compatibility between the oracle and a latent objective. We further introduce online ComPO, which retains the offline comparison mechanism and uses unlabeled policy generations for reverse-KL control relative to a reference policy. Following the coverage perspective of preference fine-tuning, we establish a performance guarantee for a basic constrained scheme under local coverage and in-distribution pairwise reward accuracy. Experiments on Mistral, Llama, Gemma-2, Qwen3, and Gemma-3 models demonstrate improvements over existing direct alignment methods, including length-controlled win rates, with pair-level diagnostics providing evidence consistent with mitigating likelihood displacement.

</details>


### [189] [Objective vs. Search: Decomposing What Makes a Good Tokeniser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19145)
*Ahmetcan Yavuz,Clara Meister,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过引入BottomUpLL与TopDownComp两种新分词算法补全2x2设计空间，解耦优化目标与搜索过程对语言模型性能的独立影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPE与UnigramLM在优化目标（压缩vs对数似然）和搜索过程（自底向上合并vs自顶向下剪枝）两个正交维度上不同，现有比较混淆了这两轴，无法判断差异来源。

Method: 提出两种新分词器完成2x2设计：自底向上似然分词器BottomUpLL与自顶向下压缩分词器TopDownComp；用各算法分词器训练语言模型，变化模型规模、词表大小与领域（纯英语vs多语言）。

Result: 在bits-per-byte指标上，搜索过程而非优化目标是主导因素，自底向上分词器在多数设置下取得更低的bits-per-byte；而在BLiMP任务上，设计选择与性能之间无一致关系。

Conclusion: 研究解耦了分词器设计选择对语言建模性能的影响，为更合理构建分词器提供具体指导。

Abstract: Two dominant tokenisation algorithms are used by modern language models: byte-pair encoding (BPE) and UnigramLM. These differ along two orthogonal axes: their optimisation objective (compression vs. log-likelihood) and their search procedure (bottom-up merging vs. top-down pruning). Existing comparisons confound these axes, making it unclear whether their observed differences stem from what is being optimised vs. how it is being optimised. We disentangle the two by introducing two new tokenisation algorithms that complete this 2x2 design space: BottomUpLL, a bottom-up likelihood-based tokeniser, and TopDownComp, a top-down compression-based tokeniser. We train language models with tokenisers produced by each algorithm, varying: model size, vocabulary sizes, and domain (English-only vs. multilingual). Evaluating models on bits-per-byte, we find that the search procedure -- not the objective -- is the dominant factor: bottom-up tokenisers consistently achieve lower bits-per-byte in most settings. Evaluating models on the BLiMP task, however, shows no consistent relationship between design choice and performance. Overall, our results disentangle the effect of tokeniser design choices on language modelling performance, offering concrete guidance for their more principled construction.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Not All Agents Are Equal: Code Quality and Post-Merge Maintenance Across Five Autonomous Coding Agents in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17598)
*Obada Kraishan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文基于37,623个来源标注的PR（来自五家商业编码代理与人类基线，覆盖2,807个仓库），测量代码安全气味、可维护性、合并后流失、回滚率与人工评审行为。结论：质量差异因厂商而异；代理代码总体安全气味更少；评审投入分布不均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码代理已在公共仓库大规模提交PR，但代码合并后的实际表现（安全性、可维护性、是否被回滚、人工评审投入）缺乏系统认识。论文旨在填补这一空白，量化代理生成代码的落地后果。

Method: 结合AIDev数据集与58,792条缓存的GitHub API响应，对2024年12月至2025年7月间五家商业代理（OpenAI Codex、Devin、GitHub Copilot、Cursor、Claude Code）与匹配的人类基线PR进行对比分析，测量新增代码的安全气味、结构可维护性、合并后churn、回滚率及人工评审行为。

Result: 三点突出发现：(1) 质量差异呈厂商特异性而非一致：Codex的PR回滚率约为人类的一半（6.1% vs 11.5%，OR=0.50），Devin的PR回滚更多（14.5%，OR=1.31）；(2) 跨厂商汇总的代理代码比人类代码更少含安全气味（OR=0.63），主要因硬编码凭据和eval类构造更少；(3) 评审投入分布不均：Copilot的PR获得最多人工评审与变更请求，Claude Code的PR等待首次人工评审最久（中位数12.6小时）。

Conclusion: 代理生成代码的合并后表现不能一概而论：安全性在汇总层面优于人类代码，但回滚风险与评审负担因厂商差异显著。研究同时公开全部流水线代码、统计报告与图表以供复现。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents now open pull requests in public repositories at a scale that was out of reach two years ago, yet little is known about what happens to that code after it lands. This paper studies 37,623 provenance-labeled pull requests (PRs) from five commercial agents (OpenAI Codex, Devin, GitHub Copilot, Cursor, and Claude Code) and a matched human baseline, drawn from 2,807 GitHub repositories between December 2024 and July 2025. We combine the AIDev dataset with 58,792 cached GitHub API responses to measure security smells in added code, structural maintainability, post-merge churn, revert rates, and human review behavior. Three results stand out. First, quality differences are vendor-specific rather than uniform: Codex-authored PRs were reverted about half as often as human PRs (6.1% vs. 11.5%, odds ratio 0.50), while Devin PRs were reverted more often (14.5%, odds ratio 1.31). Second, agent code pooled across vendors was less likely than human code to contain a security smell (odds ratio 0.63), driven by fewer hardcoded credentials and eval-style constructs. Third, review effort concentrates unevenly: Copilot PRs drew the most human reviews and change requests, and Claude Code PRs waited the longest for a first human review (median 12.6 hours). All pipeline code, statistical reports, and figures are released for replication.

</details>


### [191] [Quiescence in Concert: Composing Multi-Channel Time-Outs for IOCO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17640)
*Laura Brandán Briones,Petra van den Bos,Marcus Gerhold*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出多通道提升算子，使建模者用普通标签迁移系统即可隐式获得带独立超时的定时自动机，并证明其与并行组合可交换，且MBT的符合性、测试生成与判定得以保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时系统测试理论丰富但实践中应用不足，主要因为定时机制需要专业知识。此前工作通过canonic lifting算子让建模者用普通标签迁移系统建模，隐式获得表达静默行为（输出缺失）的定时自动机。本文进一步处理每个组件在各自通道上携带自身超时的情形。

Method: 引入多通道提升（multi-channel lifting）算子：每个组件在各自通道上拥有自己的超时。核心是证明该提升与并行组合可交换——即"先组合再提升"等价于"先提升再组合"。并分析该提升下MBT测试框架的性质保持。

Result: 证明了提升算子与并行组合可交换；MBT机制得以存续：在基于超时的测试者能观察到的可测迹（testable traces）上，符合性、测试生成和判定结果均被提升所保持。

Conclusion: 多通道提升扩展了此前的时间提升方法，使各组件可在独立通道上处理超时，同时保持与并行组合的交换性及MBT核心性质，降低了实践者使用定时测试理论的难度。

Abstract: In Model-Based Testing (MBT), test suites are generated automatically from a formal specification. The theory of testing real-time systems is rich, but often underused in practice, partly because applying the timed machinery demands expertise practitioners should not need. In prior work we addressed this for timed testing with a canonic lifting operator, which lets a modeller specify behaviour as plain labelled transition systems while implicitly obtaining the timed automata that express their quiescent behaviour (the explicit absence of outputs) with timers. This paper takes the next step: we show that our lifting still works when each component carries its own time-out on its own channel. This way, we introduce a multi-channel lifting. We show that it commutes with parallel composition, i.e. composing and lifting is the same as lifting first and then composing. We show that the MBT apparatus survives: conformance, test generation and verdicts are preserved by the lifting, on the testable traces that a time-out based tester can observe.

</details>


### [192] [CARE: A Responsibility-Oriented Architecture for Domain-Scoped Resolution in Modular and Upgradeable Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17647)
*Ali Rajabi Nekoo,Laleh Rasoul,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE——一种面向责任划分的模块化智能合约架构，通过形式化模型、TLA+语义验证与对照实验，明确了协调、解析、共享状态、可复用服务与观测等架构责任的归属边界，并引入领域范围的能力解析机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化、可升级的智能合约系统虽支持替换执行逻辑，但仅靠模块化分发无法界定各组件在架构责任（协调、解析、共享状态、可复用服务、观测）上的归属，导致责任边界模糊、演进范围难以局部化。

Method: 提出责任导向架构CARE，将执行协调与选择执行目标的权限分离，引入领域范围的能力解析；建立形式化架构模型并推导五项结构属性；用TLA+编码行为语义，并以TLC针对七项架构不变量进行验证，期间用中间结果迭代修正模型；与EIP-2535及纯路由基线开展受控实证评估，并进行针对委托执行与重入的对抗性测试。

Result: 在评测负载中，CARE引入约固定7.7k gas的运行时路由开销；局部能力替换的成本接近纯路由对照组，且显著低于所评测的EIP-2535重配置路径；对抗性测试刻画了授权控制及委托执行、重入相关的残余风险。

Conclusion: CARE适合作为实现显式责任归属与局部化演进的架构模型，而非现有智能合约可升级机制的通用替代方案。

Abstract: Modular and upgradeable smart-contract systems enable replaceable execution logic, but modular dispatch alone does not define which components own architectural responsibilities such as coordination, resolution, shared state, reusable services, and observation. This paper presents CARE, a responsibility-oriented architecture that makes these ownership boundaries explicit and introduces domain-scoped capability resolution, separating execution coordination from the authority that selects an execution target. CARE is defined through a formal architectural model from which five structural properties are derived. Its behavioral semantics are further encoded in TLA+ and evaluated with TLC against seven architectural invariants. Intermediate TLC results were used to refine the architectural model before the final validation run. A controlled empirical evaluation against EIP-2535 and a routing only baseline shows that CARE introduces an approximately fixed 7.7k-gas runtime routing premium in the evaluated workloads, while localized capability replacement remains close to the routing-only control and substantially less expensive than the evaluated EIP-2535 reconfiguration path. Adversarial tests further characterize authorization controls and residual risks associated with delegated execution and reentrancy. These results position CARE as an architectural model for explicit responsibility ownership and localized evolution rather than as a universal replacement for existing smart-contract upgradeability mechanisms

</details>


### [193] [A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Quality Assurance Practices and Gaps in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17698)
*Wuyang Dai,Moses Openja,Jiho Shin,Hung Viet Pham,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对157个开源LLM智能体项目进行了大规模实证研究，分析其质量保障实践，发现当前QA覆盖碎片化、防护不一致、测试缺乏边界与对抗场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体集成了规划、记忆、工具使用、代码执行和外部交互，自主性增强的同时引入了可靠性、安全性和安全性新风险，因此需要系统了解现有项目的质量保障实践现状。

Method: 选取157个GitHub星标数不少于100的开源LLM智能体项目，分析其文档、源代码、配置和测试，从执行面、防护措施、测试制品、风险场景和反复出现的缺口等维度刻画QA实践。

Result: 当前QA主要聚焦基础功能和高风险动作，覆盖仍较碎片化；防护措施在等效执行路径上应用不一致；测试很少考察边界、对抗或多步工具使用失败；已识别的风险很少转化为端到端QA检查。

Conclusion: 需要超越功能级测试，转向系统化的端到端验证，以确保智能体工作流在与不可信输入、工具、持久状态和外部API交互时始终处于预期边界内。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used across software engineering, web automation, research, and productivity applications. Their integration of planning, memory, tool use, code execution, and external interactions enables greater autonomy but also introduces new reliability, safety, and security risks. We present a large-scale empirical study of quality assurance (QA) practices in 157 open-source LLM-based agent projects with at least 100 GitHub stars. We analyze documentation, source code, configurations, and tests to characterize QA practices across execution surfaces, safeguards, testing artifacts, risk scenarios, and recurring gaps. We find that current QA primarily focuses on basic functionality and high-risk actions, while coverage remains fragmented. Safeguards are inconsistently applied across equivalent execution routes, tests rarely examine boundary, adversarial, or multi-step tool-use failures, and identified risks are seldom translated into end-to-end QA checks. These findings highlight the need to move beyond feature-level testing toward systematic end-to-end validation that ensures agent workflows remain within intended boundaries when interacting with untrusted inputs, tools, persistent state, and external APIs.

</details>


### [194] [Detecting Argument-Swap Bugs Using Context-Enhanced Code Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17844)
*Subrata Das,Ali Aman,Muhammad Asaduzzaman,Kawser Wazed Nafi,Salimur Choudhury*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出BugProbe，一种无需调用-定义映射、基于学习的Python方法调用参数顺序错误检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用参数名与形参名词法相似性检测参数顺序错误的方法依赖调用与定义的映射，而Python等动态类型语言中该映射难以获取。

Method: BugProbe利用局部上下文与参数使用上下文等多源信息，结合基于名称的相似性与机器学习构建参数的表征；从GitHub前1000星标仓库的132,739个Python源文件构建3,371,244个合成训练样本，并人工核验提交历史得到含55个真实参数交换bug的基准。

Result: 在该数据集上取得高准确率，在标准评价指标上持续优于最先进基线。

Conclusion: 无需显式调用-定义解析即可有效检测参数顺序bug，适合动态类型语言场景。

Abstract: Names of source code elements convey rich semantic information and have been widely used in software engineering tasks such as bug detection, code completion, type prediction, and code classification. Prior studies exploit lexical similarity between method arguments and formal parameter names to detect bugs caused by incorrectly ordered arguments, typically relying on establishing mappings between method calls and their corresponding definitions. However, such mappings are often difficult to obtain in dynamically typed languages like Python. In this paper, we present BugProbe, a learning-based approach for detecting incorrectly ordered arguments in Python method calls that does not require call-to-definition mappings. Our approach leverages multiple sources of contextual information, including local context and argument usage context, and combines name-based similarity with machine learning to construct expressive representations of method arguments. We collect a new dataset of 132,739 Python source files from the top-1,000 starred GitHub repositories, yielding 3,371,244 synthetic training examples, and contribute a curated benchmark of 55 real-world argument-swap bugs manually verified from commit histories. We evaluate our approach on this dataset and show that it achieves high accuracy and consistently outperforms a state-of-the-art baseline across standard evaluation metrics. These results demonstrate that effective detection of argument-ordering bugs is possible without relying on explicit call-to-definition resolution, making the approach well suited for dynamically typed language settings.

</details>


### [195] [Experimental Settings in LLM-Based Program Repair: A Study of Inputs, Tool Access, Feedback, and Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17993)
*Xushu Dai,Yicheng Cai,Nanqing Luo,Pei-Yu Tseng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文指出，仅凭基准名称、修复缺陷数量和验证测试无法完整刻画自动程序修复（APR）系统的评估任务；其提出一个框架与机器可读模式，从任务单元、缺陷定位假设、初始输入、工具权限、修复期反馈、最终验证与资源预算七个维度显式描述实验设置，并分析 Defects4J 与 SWE-bench 上已报告的结果，说明基准身份不足以重构被评估任务或确定跨系统比较范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期基于大语言模型的修复系统在修复前提供的信息、修复期间允许的仓库与测试操作、以及失败尝试后返回的反馈上存在差异，使同一基准可实例化为差异显著的不同修复任务（从局部补丁生成到仓库级诊断与迭代修复）。传统报告方式（基准、修复数量、验证测试）已无法完整说明系统实际面对的任务，导致复现困难和跨系统比较范围不清。

Method: 作者提出一个用于显式指定 APR 结果相关实验设置的框架，并据此分析在 Defects4J 和 SWE-bench 上评估的系统所报告的实验设置。每个结果从以下维度刻画：任务单元、故障定位假设、初始输入、工具访问权限、修复期反馈、最终验证、资源预算。同时引入一个机器可读的 schema 来规范描述每个实验设置。

Result: 分析表明，仅凭基准身份不足以重构被评估的任务，也不足以判断跨系统修复率比较的合理范围；不同系统即便在同一基准上，其实际修复任务也可能存在实质差异。

Conclusion: 作者提出机器可读的模式来指定每个实验设置，以提升可复现性，并使跨系统比较的范围变得明确。

Abstract: Evaluations of automated program repair (APR) systems commonly report the benchmark, the number of repaired defects, and the tests used for final patch validation, but these items no longer fully specify the repair task presented to a system. Recent LLM-based systems differ in the information supplied before repair, the repository and testing operations permitted during repair, and the feedback returned after unsuccessful attempts, allowing the same benchmark to instantiate substantially different repair tasks ranging from localized patch generation to repository-level diagnosis and iterative repair. We present a framework for explicitly specifying the experimental settings associated with reported APR results. We analyze reported experimental settings from systems evaluated on Defects4J and SWE-bench and characterize each result by its task unit, fault-localization assumptions, initial input, tool access, repair-time feedback, final validation, and resource budget. Our analysis shows that benchmark identity alone is insufficient to reconstruct the evaluated task or determine the appropriate scope of comparison across reported repair rates. We therefore introduce a machine-readable schema for specifying each experimental setting to improve reproducibility and make the scope of cross-system comparisons explicit.

</details>


### [196] [An Empirical Evaluation of Cost-Efficient Large Language Models on Algorithmic Programming Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18052)
*Chandimal Adikari,Nandika Herath*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究对三款低成本大语言模型在Java Spring Boot服务方法生成任务中的可靠性进行了实证评估，发现结构合规接近满分，但仅12.9%的返回答案正确，且响应可靠性与正确性呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业希望以低成本LLM自动生成生产级代码，但缺乏对其是否可信、能否按书面规格正确实现的实证证据，本研究旨在检验这一信任是否成立。

Method: 使用Gemini Flash 3、GPT-5.4 mini和Claude Haiku 4.5，针对992道算法题生成符合指定签名与DTO规范的Java Spring Boot服务方法；交叉4种模型与智能体编码工具组合及2种提示变体，共8种配置，禁止迭代与硬编码答案，并保留8道题不含输入以探测缺失输入响应。共生成7,593个方法，按八类结果分类法归类其答案产出方式，随后部署执行，获得7,936次测量请求并关联分类结果。

Result: 结构合规率接近上限，但38.4%的方法并未计算其返回的值，仅12.9%的返回答案正确；按结果类别看，响应可靠性与正确性呈反向关系，真正进行计算的方法应答率最低，正确率为19.3%。

Conclusion: 低成本LLM虽能满足形式规范，却难以保证功能正确，其"看起来可用"的响应反而更不可靠，尚不足以信任其按书面规格生成企业代码。局限包括每种配置仅单次生成、测试框架覆盖不全、单次计时、语法层面分类以及可能的语料污染。

Abstract: This study empirically evaluates whether cost-efficient Large Language Models (LLMs) can be trusted to generate enterprise code to a written specification. Three models (Gemini Flash 3, GPT-5.4 mini and Claude Haiku 4.5) were asked to solve 992 algorithmic problems as Java Spring Boot service methods conforming to a mandated signature and data-transfer-object specification, crossing four model and agentic coding tool combinations with two prompt variants to yield eight configurations, with iteration forbidden and hardcoded answers explicitly prohibited. Eight problem statements were withheld to probe how models respond to missing input. The 7,593 resulting methods were classified by an eight-class outcome taxonomy describing what each does about producing an answer, then deployed and executed, giving 7,936 measured requests joined to that classification. Structural conformance approached ceiling, yet 38.4% of methods do not compute the value they returned and only 12.9% of returned answers were correct. Conditioning on outcome class shows that response reliability and correctness are inversely related, whereas genuinely computing methods answered least often and were correct 19.3%. Limitations include single generation runs per configuration, partial harness coverage, single-pass timing, syntactic classification, and probable corpus contamination.

</details>


### [197] [Relationally Guided Use Case Modeling with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18291)
*Guangyu Wang,Bangqi Li,Ji Wu,Zhijun Shao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 FlowGen，一种基于大语言模型的完整用例流程自动构建方法，涵盖基本流程生成、分支点预测与分支条件驱动的备选流程生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用例流程是需求分析、架构与详细设计、测试用例生成等下游软件工程活动的重要支撑，但人工构建成本高且依赖专家经验；现有自动化方法在保持语义一致性、控制流逻辑、数据流逻辑及系统边界方面仍有不足，尤其是在识别分支点和生成备选流程时。

Method: FlowGen 采用基于大语言模型的语义信息处理（SIP）提取语义元素，构建由增强型 R-GAT 编码的语义关系图（SRG），用于基本流程生成（BFGen）；并通过 BPP 预测分支点，通过 AFGen 生成分支条件驱动的备选流程。

Result: 在 13 个公开数据集和 7 个工业数据集上，FlowGen 在三个核心组件上均优于竞争基线：BFGen 在 Precision 上提升 14%，Recall 提升 7–25%，F1 提升 11–30%，AUC 提升 10–19%；BPP 的 Precision 提升 30–110%，Recall 提升 33–91%，F1 提升 32–117%；AFGen 的 Precision 提升 8–23%，F1 提升 5–18%，AUC 提升 0.6–2.5%。

Conclusion: 实验验证了基于大语言模型的 SIP 模块和 BFGen 中注意力保持因子的有效性，分析了需求完整性对 BFGen 的影响，并考察了不同分支相关上下文范围对 AFGen 的作用。

Abstract: Use case flows are important elements of use case modeling because they support downstream software engineering activities, including requirements analysis, architectural and detailed design, and test case generation. However, constructing them manually is costly and expertise-intensive, while existing automated approaches still struggle to preserve semantic consistency, control-flow logic, data-flow logic, and the intended system boundary, especially when identifying branch points and generating alternative flows. To address this problem, we propose FlowGen for complete use case flow construction. FlowGen uses LLM-based Semantic Information Processing (SIP) to extract semantic elements, constructs a Semantic Relational Graph (SRG) encoded by an enhanced R-GAT for basic flow generation (BFGen), and further supports branch point prediction through BPP and branch-conditioned alternative flow generation through AFGen. Evaluations on 13 public and 7 industrial datasets show that FlowGen consistently outperforms competitive baselines in all three core components. In particular, BFGen improves over the best baseline by 14% in Precision, 7-25% in Recall, 11-30% in F1, and 10-19% in AUC; BPP improves Precision by 30-110%, Recall by 33-91%, and F1 by 32-117%; AFGen improves Precision by 8-23%, F1 by 5-18%, and AUC by 0.6-2.5%. Moreover, we validate the effectiveness of the LLM-based SIP module and the attention preservation factor in BFGen, analyze the impact of requirement completeness on BFGen, and examine how different scopes of branch-related context affect AFGen.

</details>


### [198] [A Study of the Reliability of Agentic AI-Generated Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18298)
*Ayesha Shafique,Barton P. MIller,Elisa R. Heymann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过模糊测试对比十款知名Linux工具程序的AI生成版本与人类编写版本，发现AI生成代码可靠性通常相当甚至更高，但更易出现挂起类故障，且高质量产出依赖精心设计的提示词与人工监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界声称基于智能体的AI软件开发能加快开发速度、提升程序员效率并生成更可靠的代码，但这些主张缺乏客观验证，因此本文试图以可量化、可对比的方式检验这些说法。

Method: 采用三项做法：一是使用典型的最佳实践型智能体AI工作流开发软件；二是选取十个知名、达到发布质量的人类编写的Linux实用工具程序作为对照基准；三是用模糊随机测试衡量可靠性，同时使用经典黑盒生成式测试和基于AFL++的现代覆盖率引导（灰盒变异）测试。

Result: AI生成版本的工具程序通常与最新人类版本同样可靠，甚至往往更可靠；虽然AI生成版本也存在失败，但比标准代码库中的版本更少。有趣的是，AI生成代码更少出现内存错误（如缓冲区溢出），但更容易出现挂起（如无限循环）。此外，验证表明智能体AI要生成健壮可靠的软件需要谨慎实践与人工监督，代码质量高度依赖所用提示词与技能，以及人类引导者的应对方式。

Conclusion: 智能体AI工作流（连同其提示词与技能）可成为代码的一种规格说明，从而有助于实现软件的可持续性与成本效益；但其应用仍需人工监督与规范化实践。

Abstract: Agentic-AI based software development offers the promise of faster completion of the software, greater programmer efficiency, and more reliable code. The question is how can we verify these claims in an objective way? In this project, we attempted to answer this question based on three practices. First, we applied a typical best-practices agentic AI workflow for software development. Second, our target programs were ten well-known, release-quality human-written Linux utility programs so that we could compare the AI-generated code against a concrete ground truth. Third, we based our measure of reliability on a widely used testing technique, fuzz random testing. For this testing, we used both classic black box, generational testing and more modern coverage guided (gray box, mutational) testing using AFL++. We found that the AI-generated versions of the utility programs were typically as reliable - often more reliable - than the latest human-generated versions of these programs. While the AI-generated versions did have some failures, they were less common than the code from the standard repositories. Interestingly, the AI-generated code was less likely to have failures such as memory errors (such as buffer overflows) but more likely to have hangs such as infinite loops. In addition, we verified that generating robust and reliable software using agentic AI requires careful practice and human supervision. The quality of the code is highly dependent on the prompts and skills used, and how the human directing the process responds. We also demonstrated that using agentic AI workflow for software development (with its prompts and skills) can become a specification of the code that leads to cost-effective sustainability of the software.

</details>


### [199] [A Study on the Impact of Natural Language Differences in Prompts on Automatic Code Generation Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18311)
*Haruka Tokumasu,Masanari Kondo,Alexander Serebrenik,Dong Wang,Kei Koyanagi,Kotaro Noguchi,Naoyasu Ubayashi,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究量化了输入提示的自然语言对LLM代码生成性能的影响（语言偏见），并评估了通过翻译缓解该偏见的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量基于LLM的代码生成研究，但输入提示中自然语言的影响（语言偏见）尚未被探索。本研究旨在量化该影响并评估缓解策略。

Method: 使用7个LLM（GPT-4o、o3-mini、DeepSeek-V3.2、Llama-3、Qwen2.5-Coder-14B、Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B、GitHub Copilot），在AtCoder、LeetCode、BigCodeBench上评估Accuracy，每个问题以英语、日语和中文呈现，比较翻译前后的Accuracy。

Result: 问题陈述的自然语言会影响LLM代码生成性能：各数据集官方支持的语言取得最高中位数Accuracy。翻译提升了Accuracy，但其效果在不同数据集和模型类型间并不一致。AtCoder包含较高比例的叙事式陈述和较长的陈述。

Conclusion: 自然语言显著影响LLM代码生成准确率。翻译可在某些情境下缓解语言偏见，但其有效性取决于数据集和模型类型。叙事特征和上下文长度是与语言偏见及翻译缓解效果相关的重要因素。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance in automatic code generation tasks, thereby encouraging new research in this area. Although numerous studies have explored LLM-based code generation, the impact of the natural language in input prompts remains unexplored (language bias). This study aims to (1) quantify how the natural language of input prompts influences LLM-based code generation performance and (2) evaluate a mitigation strategy to reduce language bias in code generation. We assess code generation Accuracy on AtCoder, LeetCode, and BigCodeBench. To quantify the language bias on code generation, each problem is presented in English, Japanese, and Chinese. We use seven LLMs (GPT-4o, o3-mini, DeepSeek-V3.2, Llama-3, Qwen2.5-Coder-14B, Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B, and GitHub Copilot) and assess their performance in terms of Accuracy (the number of problems for which generated code passes all test cases). We compare Accuracy before and after translation to evaluate the effectiveness of translation as a mitigation strategy. We observed that the natural language of problem statements affects LLM-based code generation performance. Specifically, the languages officially supported by each dataset achieved the highest median Accuracy. Also, translation improved Accuracy, but its effectiveness was not consistent across datasets and model types. We found that AtCoder contained a particularly high proportion of narrative-style problem statements and longer problem statements. Natural language significantly affects LLM code generation accuracy. Translation can mitigate language bias in some settings, but its effectiveness depends on the dataset and model type. Furthermore, the narrative aspects and context length of input prompts are important factors related to language bias and the effectiveness of translation as a mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [200] [From Component Snapshots to Lifecycle Traces: Agent-Based Software Composition Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18391)
*Chaofan Li,Zhengduo Xue,Chengxiang Li,Yutao Hu,Yueming Wu,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCA-Agent，一种基于智能体的生命周期感知软件成分分析（SCA）方法，通过跨 Code、Build、Release、Deploy、Runtime 五个阶段重构可溯源、有证据支撑的组件生命周期轨迹，实现组件身份、版本、引入路径、传播关系与最终状态的关联分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链安全需要准确识别第三方组件并理解其从开发到执行的演化过程。现有 SCA 方法通常只检查清单、构建环境、发布产物、容器或运行时中的某一环节，产生阶段性的局部视图。由于依赖在生命周期各阶段会被解析、移除、重打包和转换，单一快照无法同时刻画组件的来源与最终去向，而简单组合多个阶段的快照也无法解决跨阶段关系的对应问题。

Method: 提出 SCA-Agent：一种基于智能体的生命周期感知 SCA 方法。它自适应地探索项目特定的分析路径，收集各阶段专属证据，并跨阶段关联观测结果，从而恢复组件身份、版本、引入路径、传播关系以及最终生命周期状态。

Result: 在来自 Java、JavaScript 和 Python 生态的 105 个真实项目上进行评估。SCA-Agent 在所有生命周期阶段和生态系统中均取得最高的组件检测 F1。在漏洞暴露评估方面，其 F1 达到 96.69%，比最佳传统 SCA 工具高出 18.76 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，生命周期感知的 SCA 能够支持可溯源的组件来源追踪，并实现更准确的软件供应链风险评估。

Abstract: Software supply-chain security requires accurate identification of third-party components and an understanding of how they evolve from development to execution. Existing software composition analysis (SCA) approaches examine manifests, build environments, release artifacts, containers, or runtime states, but typically produce only stage-specific views of software composition. As dependencies are resolved, removed, repackaged, and transformed across lifecycle stages, a single snapshot cannot capture both where a component originates and where it ultimately ends up. Combining snapshots from multiple stages still leaves their cross-stage relationships unresolved. We present SCA-Agent, an agent-based approach to lifecycle-aware SCA that reconstructs evidence-backed component lifecycle traces across Code, Build, Release, Deploy, and Runtime. SCA-Agent adaptively explores project-specific analysis paths, gathers stage-specific evidence, and correlates observations across stages to recover component identities, versions, introduction paths, propagation relationships, and final lifecycle states. We evaluate SCA-Agent on 105 real-world projects from the Java, JavaScript, and Python ecosystems. SCA-Agent achieves the highest component detection F1 across all lifecycle stages and ecosystems. For vulnerability exposure assessment, it reaches an F1 score of 96.69%, exceeding the best traditional SCA tool by 18.76 percentage points. These results show that lifecycle-aware SCA supports traceable component provenance and more accurate software supply-chain risk assessment.

</details>


### [201] [Evaluating the Health of Open-Source Smart City Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18801)
*Rodrigo Bravo Simões,Fernando Brito e Abreu,Vasco Amaral*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文运用"开源软件健康度"概念，对多个开源智慧城市平台（SCP）进行评估，以帮助资源有限的市政部门做出采纳决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 市政部门希望借助开源智慧城市平台提升效率与生活质量，且开源方案免费、有助于数字主权、避免供应商锁定。但市政IT团队技术资源匮乏，缺乏对开源的支持会阻碍其采纳。健康的生态系统（开发者与用户协作、安装与使用手册、无漏洞且有文档的代码、测试套件等）是采纳前的重要决策因素，因此需要对这些开源平台进行健康度评估。

Method: 采用"开源软件健康度"（OSS health）概念，构建覆盖上述多项特征（如开发者与用户生态、文档、代码安全性、测试套件等）的评估维度，并据此对不同的开源智慧城市平台进行评价。

Result: 对不同开源智慧城市平台进行了基于OSS健康度的评估（摘要未给出具体评估结果数值，仅表明完成了评估工作）。

Conclusion: 开源智慧城市平台对预算有限、希望提升居民与访客生活质量的市政部门具有吸引力，但其采纳取决于生态系统的健康程度；通过OSS健康度评估可为市政部门的采纳决策提供依据。

Abstract: To manage the complexity of smart cities, a variety of smart city platforms (SCPs), both proprietary and open-source, have been proposed. Typically acting as middleware between IoT devices at a lower layer and smart services at a higher layer, these platforms simplify the management of smart cities by reifying a variety of requirements common to different municipalities. Open-source software (OSS) is typically free of charge, making open-source SCPs an attractive option for municipalities with smaller budgets that still wish to improve efficiency and quality of life for residents and visitors. Furthermore, using an OSS SCP promotes digital sovereignty, since municipalities can control where the data will reside and will be less prone to vendor lock-in.
  A lack of support for OSS hinders its adoption by municipalities' IT teams, often with scarce technical resources. An active (aka "healthy") ecosystem, including well-orchestrated developers and users, the availability of installation and user manuals, non-vulnerable and documented code, test batteries, and other artifacts, is an important decision factor before adopting an OSS SCP. In this paper, we evaluate different open-source SCPs using the concept of "OSS health", covering several of the aforementioned characteristics.

</details>


### [202] [ProgramDistill: From Interactive Web Apps to Verifiable Reference-Guided SWE Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18805)
*Jeonghye Kim,Minseon Kim,Young Jin Kim,Matheus Pereira,Marc-Alexandre Côté,Alessandro Sordoni,Xingdi Yuan,Zhengyan Shi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ProgramDistill基准，通过交互功能完整的参考应用来评测编码代理推断并实现功能的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理评测多依赖issue或指令明确指定期望行为，但实际Web开发中代理需从可运行软件中推断行为并在不完整应用中实现，缺乏相应评测基准。

Method: 将应用分解为不同粒度的功能，每个功能关联可通过gold patch执行的可重放行为；采用mine-craft-patch流水线，跨26个应用发现1,975个可重放验证行为，构建4,063个任务，无需人工干预。

Result: 在九个前沿编码代理上评测：完整应用重构中GPT-6 Astra和Claude Opus 5累计工作流成功率为49.2%和28.8%；部分重构中，随恢复深度从1增至8，成功率分别从100%降至64.0%、从96%降至32%。

Conclusion: ProgramDistill提供了可扩展、难度可控的基准，用于评测和诊断编码代理，并为未来课程式训练奠定基础。

Abstract: Coding agents are typically evaluated with desired behavior specified through issues or instructions. In practical web development, however, agents may need to infer behavior from working software and implement it in an incomplete application. We introduce ProgramDistill, a benchmark evaluating coding agents on features discovered through interaction with fully functional reference applications. We build ProgramDistill by factorizing applications into features of different granularities, each associated with replayable behaviors executable via its gold patch. Our pipeline, mine-craft-patch, discovers 1,975 replay-verified behaviors across 26 applications and constructs 4,063 tasks without human intervention. Across nine frontier coding agents, GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5 achieve 49.2% and 28.8% success on cumulative workflows in full-application reconstruction. In partial-application reconstruction, success falls from 100% to 64.0% and from 96% to 32% as restoration depth increases from 1 to 8. ProgramDistill thus provides a scalable benchmark with controlled difficulty for evaluating and diagnosing coding agents, and a natural basis for future curriculum-based training.

</details>


### [203] [Removing Noise or Introducing Bias? The Hidden Cost of MSR Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18953)
*Mohit Kaushik,Jyoti Bawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析157万个GitHub仓库，揭示MSR研究中常用过滤阈值引入的维护偏差、生态系统/治理偏好偏差与关系偏差，并建议采用分层采样与优化噪声检测标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub等OSS平台是MSR研究的主要数据源，但仓库质量参差不齐，研究者常用各类过滤阈值剔除噪声，这可能从根本上改变样本人口特征，带来隐藏偏差。作者希望揭示这些任意阈值或标准所导致的偏差成本。

Method: 从SEART平台分析1.57 million（157万）个仓库，依据MSR研究中常用的阈值构建多个数据集，考察过滤过程对样本构成、变量关系的影响。

Result: 发现过滤过程存在维护偏差，掩盖了OSS项目真实的废弃率（73.42%）；过滤策略偏好某些生态系统和治理风格；采样策略还扭曲了变量之间的关系，产生关系偏差。

Conclusion: 为规避上述偏差，研究者应转向分层采样（stratified sampling），并改进噪声检测标准，以提升样本代表性与研究结论的可靠性。

Abstract: OSS platforms like GitHub serve as a primary data source for MSR research. As the platform is widely used by different users, spanning from student to developer, not all repositories are actual engineered projects. Therefore, to avoid such noise, researchers often apply several criteria, which may fundamentally change the sample demographics. To understand such biases, this study aims to uncover the hidden cost originating from these arbitrary thresholds or criteria. We analyzed 1.57 million repositories from the SEART platform and constructed several datasets from the thresholds often applied in MSR research. We identify the maintenance bias in these filtering processes, which masks the true abandonment (73.42%) realities of OSS projects. Also, these strategies favor some ecosystems and governance styles. Moreover, the sampling strategy also distorts the relationship between variables, suffering from relational biases. Therefore, to avoid such biases, the researchers should shift towards stratified sampling and refining the criteria for noise detection.

</details>


### [204] [Code Consistency Preference Optimization Verification for Language Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19002)
*Yunlong Tan,Mingqiao Mo,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于执行一致性偏好优化（CCPO）的方法，通过依赖图捕捉推理步骤的逻辑依赖与执行一致性，在数学与科学推理任务上显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Bradley-Terry奖励模型的偏好优化方法无法捕捉科学任务所需的逻辑依赖与执行一致性，制约了执行式验证在大模型数学推理中的效果。

Method: 构建含UltraFeedback提示、模型生成、验证与一致性结果的科学推理数据集；提取推理步骤表达式、前置条件与可推导关系构建依赖图，计算执行一致性分数并附加到每步生成配对训练数据；微调Llama-3-8B与DeepSeekMath-7B，并扩展Scientific Feasibility Control框架。

Result: MATH提升+17.0%，GSM8K提升+15.1%；在PhyX多模态物理推理上达50.1%准确率，超越DeepSeek-R1（49.8%）与OpenAI o3-mini（48.2%）；alpha=0.10时科学有效性覆盖率达91.7%，科学定律违反减少73%。

Conclusion: 依赖图驱动的执行一致性偏好优化能有效提升大模型数学与科学推理能力，形成CCPO模型家族，兼具准确率与科学有效性的双重优势。

Abstract: Execution-based verification enhances large language models' mathematical reasoning through computational soundness and dependency-aware filtering. However, prior preference optimization methods relying on Bradley-Terry reward models fail to capture the logical dependencies and execution consistency needed for scientific tasks. We propose a method that generates computationally sound solutions with dependency graphs for execution-consistent preference optimization. We first build a scientific reasoning dataset using UltraFeedback prompts, model generations, verification, and consistency results. Then we extract reasoning step expressions, prerequisites, and derivability relationships to construct dependency graphs and compute execution consistency scores. These scores are appended to each step, creating paired training data. Fine-tuning Llama-3-8B and DeepSeekMath-7B yields significant gains: +17.0% on MATH and +15.1% on GSM8K. Extending our Scientific Feasibility Control framework achieves 50.1% accuracy on PhyX multimodal physics reasoning, surpassing DeepSeek-R1 (49.8%) and OpenAI o3-mini (48.2%), with 91.7% scientific validity coverage at alpha=0.10 and 73% fewer scientific law violations, resulting in the CCPO model family.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [ABM-SIRTEM: A Hybrid Agent-Based and Epidemiological Model for Pandemic Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18223)
*Sheryl Paul,Samuel Williams,Preetom K. Biswas,Giulia Pedrielli,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出ABM-SIRTEM混合模型，将职业类别、经济生产力与个体福利纳入传染病建模，并动态刻画对政府干预的遵从行为，用美国四州检测数据校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COVID-19对低社会经济群体及依赖互动型职业的人群冲击更大，监管机构需在保护公共健康与减少经济扰动之间权衡。既有SIR等群体模型简化个体异质性，而细致的ABM又因个体与交互数量增加而计算昂贵。

Method: 构建ABM-SIRTEM混合模型：在个体层面引入职业类别、经济生产力与福利，同时动态建模个体对政府干预措施的遵从程度；使用美国四个州的历史阳性与阴性检测计数对模型进行校准，并考察由此产生的遵从动态。

Result: 模型成功用四州阳性和阴性检测数据完成校准，并刻画了相应的遵从行为动态，展示了疾病传播与社会经济行为之间的相互作用。

Conclusion: 该框架为在大流行应对规划中研究疾病传播与社会经济行为的交互提供了基础。

Abstract: The COVID-19 pandemic has had profound impacts on global health, social structures, and economies. It disproportionately affected lower socioeconomic groups and those reliant on interaction-based jobs. Regulatory bodies faced the challenge of designing policies that preserve public health while limiting disruption to economic stability and productivity. Epidemiological models such as SIR and agent-based models (ABMs) have been used to study disease dynamics and the socioeconomic impacts of disease and interventions. Population-level models often simplify individual heterogeneity, while detailed ABMs can become computationally expensive as the numbers of agents and interactions increase. We propose ABM-SIRTEM, a hybrid model that incorporates occupation categories, economic productivity, and welfare at the individual level while dynamically modeling compliance with government interventions. We calibrate the model against historical positive and negative test counts from four U.S. states and examine the resulting compliance dynamics. This framework provides a basis for studying the interaction between disease spread and socioeconomic behavior in pandemic-response planning.

</details>


### [206] [Social Laws for Multi-agent Coordination in Stochastic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18929)
*Rolando Fernandez,Caleb Probine,Tyler Lee,Jeffrey Chen,Erez Karpas,Muhammad Arrasy Rahman,Peter Stone,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文将社会法则概念从确定性、基于目标的多智能体环境扩展到随机、基于奖励的环境，提出α-鲁棒性度量及基于MDP归约的鲁棒性验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体环境中，协调智能体以避免干扰并保证个体稳健表现是关键挑战。既有社会法则研究局限于确定性、基于目标的设定，缺乏对随机、奖励型环境的支持。

Method: 提出社会法则在随机、奖励型环境中的形式化定义；引入α-鲁棒性概念，衡量所有智能体遵守社会法则时个体在追求最优单智能体策略下所保证保留的效用；通过归约为求解一系列马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）进行鲁棒性验证。

Result: 在玩具环境上的实验评估表明了该框架的潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作将社会法则推广至随机、基于奖励的多智能体环境，并提供可操作的鲁棒性验证途径，实验初步验证了框架的可行性。

Abstract: In multi-agent environments, coordinating agents to prevent interference and ensure robust individual performance is a critical challenge. Previous research on social laws for multi-agent systems has primarily focused on deterministic, goal-based settings. This paper extends the concept of social laws to stochastic, reward-based environments, proposing a formalism for defining and verifying their robustness under various conditions. We introduce the notion of $α$-robustness, a measure of the guaranteed utility each agent retains while pursuing its optimal single agent policy, assuming all agents obey the social law. We then present an approach for robustness verification of social laws in stochastic settings, based on a reduction to solving a series of Markov decision processes. Empirical evaluations on toy environments illustrate the potential of our framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [Making AI-Assisted Claims Independently Challengeable: Publication Authority and a Protocol for Falsifiable Publication Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17631)
*Torsten Olivi Tiltack,Yifei Dong,Kun Yu,Xu Wang,Wei Liu,Jianlong Zhou,Ren Ping Liu,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"出版权威"(Publication Authority)概念,即一种精确状态、不可转移、单次使用的出版能力,并将其实例化为 PAC-2026(Publication-Accountability Calculus)协议候选(AIJIM Protocol 候选)。评估其第四次有界语义冻结(SF-4),通过六项义务、身份向量、对抗案例、有限模型与历史实现进行验证。结果显示内部一致性、有界安全性、故障敏感性和有限可构造性得到支持,但不支持事实真理性、通用精化、盲互操作性、现场效力或标准地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助的论断在证据、分析、人类授权、呈现和更正历史指向不同状态时,可能显得权威可信。溯源、认证和透明度虽能暴露历史,但单独无法界定本文所考察的"出版转换"。因此需要一个能精确刻画"何时一次出版转换被有效授权"的机制。

Method: 提出"出版权威"作为精确状态、不可转移、单次使用的出版能力;实例化为 PAC-2026(AIJIM Protocol 候选)。定义六项义务:证据、运行与工件、测量披露、授权、表面一致性、生命周期连续性,每项产生目标绑定见证、局部反例或局部不可验证性,互不补偿;仅当存在新鲜、完整的全通过记录时才派生许可,并由一次原子出版转换消耗。使用身份向量、对抗案例、有限模型和历史实现进行评估。

Result: 十个模型探索了110,764个安全可达状态;76个不安全配置产生了预期的违规或观察者反模型。读者表面即使通过一致性检查也不能授权出版,除非接受的记录允许该表面。SF-4 将证据视野与验证时间分离,并拒绝真实但因果无效的授权。历史前驱路径复现了17个冻结的授权-后继结果;后续内部盲实例测试匹配全部183项评分预期;同主机包执行复现其240项归档观察。

Conclusion: 结果支持内部一致性、有界安全性、故障敏感性和有限可构造性,但不支持事实真理性、通用精化、盲互操作性、现场效力或标准地位。

Abstract: AI-assisted claims can appear authoritative when evidence, analysis, human authorization, presentation, and correction history refer to different states. Provenance, attestation, and transparency expose history but alone do not specify the publication transition examined here. We develop Publication Authority as an exact-state, non-transferable, single-use publication capability and instantiate it in PAC-2026 (Publication-Accountability Calculus), a machine-readable AIJIM Protocol candidate. We evaluate its fourth bounded semantic freeze (SF-4), a fixed-profile specification designed for replaceable bindings. Six obligations govern evidence, runs and artifacts, measurement disclosure, authorization, surface correspondence, and lifecycle continuity. Each yields a target-bound witness, localized counterexample, or localized unverifiability; none can compensate for another. Only a fresh, complete all-pass record derives the permit consumed by one atomic publication transition. We use identity vectors, adversarial cases, finite models, and historical implementations. Ten models explored 110,764 safe reachable states; 76 unsafe configurations produced the expected violation or observer countermodel. A reader surface passing its correspondence check cannot authorize publication unless the accepted record admits that surface. SF-4 separates evidence horizon from verification time and rejects an authentic but causally invalid authorization. A historical predecessor path reproduced 17 frozen authorization-successor outcomes. A later in-house, instance-blind test of known case classes matched all 183 scored expectations; same-host package execution reproduced its 240 archived observations. Results support internal coherence, bounded safety, fault sensitivity, and limited constructibility, but not factual truth, general refinement, blind interoperability, field efficacy, or standards status.

</details>


### [208] [EvolveTrade: Experience-Driven Policy Refinement for Self-Evolving LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17632)
*Sehee Kim,Yumin Choi,Minki Kang,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvolveTrade 将工具型交易智能体的系统提示视为文本参数化策略，通过 Policy Agent 依据决策轨迹与组合反馈进行自演化，提升多市场环境下的夏普比率与累计收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 交易智能体的工具使用策略多为部署前写死的静态规则，难以在变化的市场环境中自适应地调整证据收集、工具调用、信号验证与风险管理方式。

Method: 提出 EvolveTrade 自演化框架：把工具型交易智能体的系统提示作为文本参数化策略；每个更新区间后，Policy Agent 利用累积的决策轨迹与已实现组合反馈修订该策略，主干 LLM 保持固定，更新后的策略用于下一批交易决策。

Result: 在多种市场环境和两种 LLM 主干上的实验显示，EvolveTrade 在多数评估设置中相对固定策略基线提升了夏普比率与累计收益；行为分析表明自演化策略增加了代码介导的分析并激活与市场环境相关的计算，案例级策略-收益归因追踪了策略引起的配置变化对收益差异的贡献。

Conclusion: 自适应地改进支配工具使用的可复用流程，是构建更稳健 LLM 交易智能体的关键方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) trading agents can combine market data, news, and executable analysis, but their behavior is often controlled by static hand-written tool-use policies that are fixed before deployment. This limits their ability to adapt how they gather evidence, invoke tools, verify signals, and manage risk under changing market regimes. We introduce EvolveTrade, a self-evolving framework that treats the system prompt of a tool-using trading agent as a text-parameterized policy. After each update interval, a Policy Agent revises this policy using accumulated decision traces and realized portfolio feedback, while keeping the backbone LLM fixed. The updated policy is then used for the next batch of trading decisions, enabling the agent to refine its information-acquisition and portfolio-construction procedure over time. Experiments across multiple market regimes and two LLM backbones show that EvolveTrade often improves Sharpe Ratio and Cumulative Return over fixed-policy LLM baselines, achieving the improved SR and CR in most evaluated settings. Behavioral analyses further show that self-evolved policies increase code-mediated analysis and activate regime-relevant computations; case-level policy-to-return attributions trace how policy-induced allocation changes contribute to realized return differences. These results suggest that adapting the reusable procedure governing tool use is a key direction for building more robust LLM trading agents.

</details>


### [209] [One Color Preprocessing Improves DSATUR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17633)
*Adam Nouira,Lucas Isenmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SSLD方法，用半定规划预处理第一个颜色类后再由DSATUR完成剩余着色，在1600多个图上匹配或优于DSATUR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图着色问题（GCP）是NP难问题，DSATUR是其中最快的启发式算法之一，但通常使用比先进算法更多的颜色。作者希望在不放弃DSATUR效率的前提下提升其着色质量。

Method: SSLD（Semidefinite Spectral Learning with DSATUR）：先用类似计算Lovász theta数的半定规划（SDP）预处理出一个优质颜色类，再让DSATUR完成图的其余着色。这是首个通过固定颜色类预处理来改进DSATUR的方法。

Result: 在DIMACS实例、随机图（Erdős–Rényi、Watts-Strogatz、Barabási–Albert）、频率分配和作业车间调度等超过1600个基准实例上，SSLD在几乎所有情况下匹配或优于DSATUR，并优于朴素的GISD基线；但运行时间约为DSATUR的195倍。

Conclusion: SDP引导的首个颜色类预处理能够带来质量提升，验证了该方法方向的有效性，虽运行成本较高，但为未来改进提供了方向。

Abstract: The Graph Coloring Problem (GCP) is NP-hard and DSATUR stands as one of the fastest heuristics for it despite producing colorings that typically use more colors than state-of-the-art coloring algorithms. We propose SSLD (Semidefinite Spectral Learning with DSATUR), which improves DSATUR by preprocessing a first good color class before letting DSATUR complete coloring the rest of the given graph. We obtain this color class from a Semidefinite Programming (SDP), similar to an SDP used to compute the Lovász theta number. To the best of our knowledge, SSLD is the first approach to improve DSATUR by preprocessing through fixed color classes. We evaluate SSLD against DSATUR and against a naive 1-color-class preprocessing algorithm on DIMACS instances, random graphs (Erdős--Rényi, Watts-Strogatz, Barabási--Albert), Frequency Assignment and Job Shop Scheduling instances. SSLD matches or beats DSATUR in almost every case across over 1600 benchmark instances, and out performs the naive GISD baseline, allows us to confirm the value brought by the SDP-guided choice of the first color class. This quality comes at a runtime cost of roughly 195 times slower that DSATUR, but demonstrating that SDP-guided preprocessing of a first color class is a direction for future improvements.

</details>


### [210] [ERPBench: A State-Grounded Evaluation Paradigm for Computer-Use Agents in Enterprise Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17885)
*Kratika Bhagtani,Kusha Sridhar,Maziyar Baran Pouyan,Yuying Zhao,Eugene Siow*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ERPBench，一个在实时可复现ERP系统上评估截图型计算机使用代理的基准，并以数据库真值评分，揭示通用GUI能力无法迁移到企业级可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用代理评估集中于通用桌面与网页任务，而ERP系统（财务、采购、库存、客户运营）具有界面密集、多步协同、错误会改动持久业务记录等特点，且现有企业基准依赖专有平台或仿真近似，缺乏真实可复现的评估环境。

Method: 构建ERPBench基准，在实时且可复现的ERP系统上评估仅依赖截图的代理，任务得分与数据库中的真值比对；同时提供一个生产级执行框架，在人工审批下限制代理动作以支持安全部署，而ERPBench则以自主模式运行。

Result: 评估六个闭源与开源代理后发现，强大的通用GUI性能无法转化为企业级可靠性。即便代理进入正确表单并保存，存储记录仍常出错：部分代理在高达85%的运行中完成保存，但写入正确值的比例低至3%。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了企业工作流特有的失败模式，表明当前代理在企业环境中的可靠性存在显著缺口，需要针对企业场景的专门评估与改进。

Abstract: Computer-use agents that operate through screenshots and simulated actions are advancing rapidly, yet their evaluation remains anchored to general desktop and web tasks. Enterprise Resource Planning (ERP) systems run the finance, procurement, inventory, and customer operations of organizations worldwide, and pose distinct challenges for computer-use agents: dense interfaces, coordinated multi-step interactions, and errors that alter persistent business records rather than surfacing on screen. Existing enterprise benchmarks rely on proprietary platforms or on simulated approximations of such software. We introduce ERPBench, a benchmark that evaluates screenshot-only agents on a live and reproducible ERP system and scores each task against ground-truth values in its database. Beyond the benchmark, we present a production-grade harness that gates agent actions behind human approval for safe deployment, which ERPBench runs autonomously. Evaluating six closed and open-source agents, we demonstrate that strong general GUI performance does not transfer to enterprise reliability. Even when an agent reaches the right form and saves it, the stored record is often wrong: some agents save in up to 85% of runs but write the correct value in as few as 3%. We further characterize failure modes specific to enterprise workflows.

</details>


### [211] [Physics-Constrained Digital Twins for Sensor Integrity in Urban Pedestrian Flow: Detecting Stealthy False Data Injection with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17635)
*Oscar Mogollon Gutierrez,Fatemeh Ghasemi,Mohammadhossein Homaei,Andres Caro,Mar Avila*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究城市行人计数数据被隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击时的后果，提出基于数字孪生与流守恒定律的检测与防护框架，并在墨尔本六年数据上验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市行人计数系统已用于经济指标、规划决策和安全运营，但其上的数字孪生把传入数据流当作真值。本文研究数据并非真值时会怎样，并指出城市行人感知中“潜在流到观测”的映射比电力、水网更严重地秩亏，因此需要专门刻画隐蔽攻击。

Method: 形式化城市级行人感知的隐蔽虚假数据注入；孪生估计行人街道图上的有向流，通过学习到的图局部增益同化计数，并以耦合计量与未计量路段的流守恒残差训练；检测结合新息与残差，报警阈值用自适应共形校准设定；定义“攻击裕度”（相对降低最坏情况下流场估计破坏程度），并用白盒对手直接通过孪生优化来度量物理约束带来的收益。

Result: 在墨尔本六年数据上，仅1.18%可步行路段被计量时，单个设备被攻陷的攻击裕度达0.54；三分之一车队被攻陷时降至0.19；将街道图替换为距离图后裕度崩塌至0.09，说明收益来自守恒定律而非局部性。

Conclusion: 流守恒约束是提升城市行人感知孪生抗隐蔽虚假数据注入能力的关键来源，自适应共形校准可替代人工设定阈值，但攻击面扩大时防护效果显著下降。

Abstract: City pedestrian counting systems now feed economic indicators, planning decisions and safety operations, yet the twins built on top of them treat the incoming stream as ground truth. We study what happens when it is not. We formalise stealthy false data injection for city-scale pedestrian sensing, where the map from latent flow to observation is far more rank deficient than in the power and water networks for which stealth has been characterised. Our twin estimates directed flows on the pedestrian street graph, assimilates counts through a learned graph-localised gain, and is trained against a flow conservation residual that couples metered and unmetered segments. Detection combines the innovation with that residual, and the alarm threshold is set by adaptive conformal calibration rather than by hand. To measure what the physics buys, we define the attack margin, the relative reduction in worst-case corruption of the estimated flow field, achieved against a white-box adversary that optimises directly through the twin. On six years of Melbourne data the margin reaches 0.54 against a single compromised device and falls to 0.19 when a third of the fleet is compromised, on a network where only 1.18 per cent of walkable segments are metered. Replacing the street graph by a distance graph collapses it to 0.09, which shows that the gain comes from the conservation law rather than from locality.

</details>


### [212] [Where Should Agents Live? Energy-Memory Characterization of Agentic AI for the Edge-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18283)
*Carolina Fortuna,Vid Hanžel,Tim Strnad,Blaž Bertalanič*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 agentic-eCAL 指标，将 AI 生命周期能耗度量推广到多智能体有向工作流，结合两速率单次调用能耗模型与 7 层 OSI 数据传输，基于 A100/H100 等基准验证并研究边缘-云工作流放置问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信网络向自主 5G-Advanced/6G 演进，基于 LLM 的智能体工作流广泛部署于边缘-云连续体，而 LLM 能耗与内存开销远超人脑约 20W 的代谢预算。现有 AI 生命周期指标仅评估孤立的单模型推理，忽视多智能体执行图，导致运营商无法判断分布式智能体通信的能耗成本及智能体团队应部署在哪一层。

Method: 提出 agentic-eCAL，将 Energy Cost of AI Lifecycle (eCAL) 指标推广到有向多智能体工作流：耦合闭式两速率单次调用能耗模型（计算受限的 prefill 与内存受限的 decode）与 7 层 OSI 数据传输模型。基于 NVIDIA A100 与 H100 上数百组 GPU 基准配置、16 个开放权重模型与 8 种编排拓扑验证指标各组件，并研究工作流放置的影响。

Result: 跨 5G RAN、城域与光链路，智能体间文本传输仅占工作流总能耗的 0.25%。

Conclusion: 在边缘-云智能体放置中，分布式的能耗主导成本通常不是智能体间文本传输本身，而是该通信所引发的额外推理与上下文处理开销。

Abstract: As telecommunication networks evolve toward autonomous 5G-Advanced and 6G operations, agentic artificial intelligence (AI) workflows, where large language models (LLMs) execute multi-step reasoning, invoke diagnostic tools, retrieve domain knowledge, and coordinate across agent teams, are increasingly embedded across the edge-cloud continuum. While the biological brain accomplishes complex cognition on an exceptionally modest metabolic power budget of approximately 20W contemporary LLMs are profoundly energy- and memory-intensive, making sustainable lifecycle orchestration a critical operational priority. However, existing AI lifecycle metrics evaluate only isolated, single-model inferences or overlook multi-agent execution graphs entirely.
