Result: 在未公开的程序化 roguelike 上，留出成功率跨度为 0–86%，并显现明显的代际门槛：新一代系统的每个会话都优于上一代最佳会话。在完整游戏规模上，编译出的原始 API 控制器击败所有公平的《星际争霸 II》内置 AI 及两个作弊变体；单会话程序在留出种子上以全面征服赢得完整《文明》（Freeciv）对局，尽管对阵新手 AI 的成功率不高。

Conclusion: 这是首个无需逐回合模型调用、无需手工战术层即可在完整游戏级别独立取胜的语言智能体系统，表明前沿编程智能体开始具备长时程策略能力；冻结程序可检查，作者称之为"编译式能动性"——把开发经验编译为模型自建架构的持久可执行智能体。

Abstract: LLM agents have repeatedly struggled to convert knowledge of a game into competent play, even when researchers build the agent around the model - supplying perception, memory, skill libraries, planners, or executable-policy scaffolds. Rapid progress in coding agents raises two sharper questions: can frontier models now win games at all, and can they win them unaided, building the entire player themselves? We introduce Gauntlet, a develop-freeze-evaluate framework that ports games from small arcades to full commercial-scale titles, behind one deliberately bare contract: a general-purpose coding agent receives a game description, a raw observation/action interface, and an empty policy file - no strategy, no algorithm, no architecture. In a single autonomous session the agent experiments with the live game and engineers a standalone controller; we freeze the result and score it on held-out instances with zero model calls during play. On an unpublished procedural roguelike, held-out success spans 0-86 percent and exposes a sharp generational threshold: every observed session of a newest-generation system outperforms the best session of its predecessor. At full-game scale, a compiled raw-API controller defeats every fair StarCraft II built-in AI and two cheating variants, and single-session programs win complete games of Civilization (Freeciv) by total conquest on held-out seeds. Though at modest rates against novice AI, this is a first: no prior language-agent system had won full games of this genre standalone, without per-turn model calls and a hand-crafted tactical layer. Frontier coding agents begin to track long-horizon strategy. The frozen programs are inspectable. We call this capability compiled agency: development experience compiled into a persistent executable agent whose architecture is built by the model.

</details>


### [285] [MUSE: Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on Multi-Modal Understanding in Situated Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19088)
*Luyao Zhu,Xun Wei Yee,Wei Li,Mun Thye Mak,Wee Siong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MUSE 基准，用于评估大型视觉语言模型在情境化教育应用中的艺术图像理解能力，覆盖12项任务并揭示模型在情感解读与组合推理上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在多模态理解上进展显著，但在教育场景中的能力评估仍不充分。AI辅助语言学习需要模型理解艺术图像的语义、情感与文化内容并推理视觉语境，然而现有基准多聚焦真实世界图像或特定领域教育推理，对艺术教育内容覆盖有限。

Method: MUSE 将图像标注与问题生成解耦，从而在降低标注成本的同时支持多样化、难度可控的任务。基准包含12项任务，涵盖视觉感知、语义与情感解读、文化理解及组合推理，并精心选取多样艺术图像，聚焦新加坡与东南亚多元文化语境，兼及西方艺术传统，覆盖多主题与多难度层级。

Result: 对开源与闭源模型的评估显示，各能力维度间存在显著差异，尤其在情感解读与组合推理方面差距明显；分析进一步识别出常见失败模式及构建可信教育多模态模型的关键挑战。

Conclusion: MUSE 有望成为推动情境化教育应用中多模态理解研究的标准化基准。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable progress in multi-modal understanding, yet their capabilities in educational settings remain insufficiently evaluated. In AI-assisted language learning, models must interpret artistic imagery, understand its semantic, affective, and cultural content, and reason about visual context to support meaningful interaction. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on real-world images or domain-specific educational reasoning, providing limited coverage of artistic educational content. To address this gap, we introduce MUSE, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models on artistic image understanding in situated educational applications. MUSE decouples image annotation from question generation, enabling diverse tasks with controllable difficulty while reducing annotation effort. It comprises twelve tasks spanning visual perception, semantic and affective interpretation, culture understanding, and compositional reasoning, together with diverse artistic images deliberately curated to center Singaporean and Southeast Asian multicultural contexts alongside Western art traditions, covering multiple themes and difficulty levels. Evaluation of open-source and proprietary models reveals substantial disparities across capability dimensions, particularly in affective interpretation and compositional reasoning. Our analysis further identifies common failure modes and key challenges for developing trustworthy multi-modal models for education. We hope MUSE will serve as a standardized benchmark for advancing multi-modal understanding in situated educational applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [State Without a Landlord: An Architecture Proposal for Peer-to-Peer Replication of Durable Workflow State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17645)
*Luca Maraschi,Matteo Collina*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨将持久执行框架的日志改为工作流各方间复制的、经认证的仅追加日志，并提出基于单写者纪元核链、仲裁最终化闭包证书、显式证书驱动决策及内容寻址分发平面的架构，给出威胁模型、类型化构件与不变量，并规划验证实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前持久执行框架的日志通常由运行托管方持有，成为工作流权威状态，这使各方依赖对托管方的隐式信任。作者希望研究若日志改为工作流参与方之间复制的经认证仅追加日志，系统性质会发生何种变化。

Method: 提出一种架构：运行由单写者纪元核链组成；执行者继任由仲裁最终化的闭包证书管理；持久性、超时与检查点决策由显式证书承载而非隐式信任；大载荷通过独立的内容寻址分发平面传输。将设计表述为类型化构件与不变量集合，并用威胁模型区分签名可确立的作者身份与顺序，与其不能确立的记录效果真实性。

Result: 论文区分了哪些主张已由现有系统确立、哪些属于尚需验证的提案，并定义了原型实验，包括具体对抗性案例，以验证或证伪该提案的核心机制。摘要未报告实证结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将持久执行的日志从托管方私有的权威状态重构为参与方间可认证、可复制的仅追加日志，并通过证书化机制明确信任边界；其核心贡献是提出设计框架、不变量和可证伪的验证路径，而非给出已完成系统的性能结论。

Abstract: Durable-execution frameworks commonly journal execution steps of a long-running function and reconstruct state by deterministic replay; the journal is therefore the workflow's authoritative state, and in current deployments it typically resides with whichever provider hosts the run. This paper examines what changes if the journal is instead an authenticated, append-only log replicated among the parties to the workflow. We propose an architecture in which runs are chains of single-writer epoch cores; executor succession is governed by quorum-finalized closure certificates; durability, timeout, and checkpoint decisions are carried by explicit certificates rather than implicit trust; and large payloads travel a separate content-addressed distribution plane. A threat model separates what signatures establish (authorship and order) from what they do not (truth of recorded effects). We state the design as a set of typed artifacts and invariants, identify which claims are established by existing systems and which are proposals requiring validation, and define the prototype experiments, including specific adversarial cases, that would validate or falsify the proposal's central mechanisms.

</details>


### [287] [Trust propagation and structural containment in Multi-agent LLM pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17648)
*Tanzim Hossain Safin,Sharif Noor Zisad,Swakkhar Shatabda,Ragib Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体LLM系统中的攻击传播，提出轻量级"Observer+授权层"方案，在四智能体LangGraph流水线中同时隔离未授权执行并降低误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统常由不同权限等级的智能体协作，低权限智能体被攻陷后可影响高权限智能体，触发未授权操作。作者希望实证刻画这类攻击的传播路径，并检验结构性授权能否在上游LLM判断失效时仍约束执行。

Method: 构建含Supervisor、Researcher、Validator、Executor的四智能体LangGraph流水线，评估共享内存投毒与通过检索文档中伪造审批实施的间接提示注入。将Validator判断与独立授权层对比，后者使用任务绑定签名令牌及独立验证的策略预言机；并引入Observer层来减少智能体劫持误报。

Result: 在3个随机种子、60个标注任务上，内存投毒在无防御时全部到达执行。启用授权后，Judgment Bypass Rate（JBR）达100%但Unsafe Action Rate为0%，说明Validator虽被攻陷而执行被约束。面对持有签名密钥的攻击者，策略预言机实现观察到遏制，独立编写的最小权限策略仍保持该结果。Observer层将智能体劫持误报率从49%降至7%，不削弱执行级安全。

Conclusion: 结构性授权可以在上游LLM判断失效时遏制被攻陷智能体的行为；即使Validator被绕过，执行层仍可保持安全，并且引入Observer可在不牺牲执行安全的前提下显著降低误报。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly automate tasks involving agents with different levels of privilege, creating a security risk in which a compromised low-privilege agent can influence a higher-privilege agent and trigger an unauthorized action. We study attack propagation in a four-agent LangGraph pipeline comprising a Supervisor, Researcher, Validator, and Executor. We evaluate shared-memory poisoning and indirect prompt injection through a forged approval embedded in a retrieved document. We compare the Validator's judgment with an independent authorization layer using task-bound signed tokens and a separately verified policy oracle. Our contribution is an empirical study of attack propagation, a component-level ablation of the authorization boundary, and the Judgment Bypass Rate (JBR), which measures compromise at the attacked agent rather than at the final action. Across three seeds and 60 labeled tasks, memory poisoning reaches execution in every undefended trial. With authorization enabled, it achieves 100% JBR but 0% Unsafe Action Rate, showing that the Validator can remain compromised while execution is contained. Against an attacker possessing the signing secret, the policy oracle provides the observed containment, while an independently authored least privilege policy preserves this result. An Observer layer reduces the false-positive rate for agent hijacking from 49% to 7% without weakening execution-level security. These results show that structural authorization can contain compromised agent behavior even when upstream LLM judgment fails.

</details>


### [288] [Reflections on Trusting Trust, Revisited: Contaminating Self-Modifying AI Coding Agents with Poisoned Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17817)
*Franziska Roesner,Tadayoshi Kohno*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将 Thompson 的"信任信任"编译器后门攻击推广到自我修改型 AI 编码代理：攻击者通过污染代理自评估/自改进所依赖的基准，可诱导其后代在干净、留出的任务上写出有漏洞的代码，并在三个自我修改编码代理上验证了这一攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thompson 的"Reflections on Trusting Trust"表明编译器可被投毒以重新植入自身后门，即便重新编译干净源码也无法消除。如今大量编码工作由 AI 编码代理完成，且这些代理越来越多地生成自身的新版本。作者由此提出疑问：当"编译器"变为自我修改的编码代理时，攻击面是否会转移。核心动机是考察敌方能否通过向代理的自评估与自改进流程提供被投毒的基准，从而诱导未来的代理版本在干净、留出的任务上生成有漏洞的代码。

Method: 作者将 Thompson 式攻击实例化到三个近期提出的自我修改编码代理上：Darwin Gödel Machine（作者对其做了实验性修改）、Self-Improving Coding Agent，以及 Hyperagents（后两者基本未加修改）。攻击方式为向代理的自评估/自改进流程注入被投毒的基准，观察其是否会被继承并泛化到干净任务上。作者还进行了对照实验，将已投毒的代理再针对干净基准进行演化，以检验污染的持久性，并从实验中提炼出使基准投毒攻击成立所需的漏洞、基准、模型与代理脚手架等方面的充分条件。

Result: 实验取得了成功的概念验证。例如，在使用 Sonnet 4.5 驱动的 Hyperagents 上，投毒基准使代理自我演化出会关闭 HTTPS 证书校验的指令，且这一行为在中性的 URL 抓取任务上被触发。此外，作者发现即便随后让被投毒的代理针对干净基准继续演化，污染往往仍然持续存在。

Conclusion: 自我修改型编码代理对基准投毒攻击具有可被利用的脆弱性，且污染可以在后续演化中对干净基准保持持久。作者讨论了若干防御方向，并主张自我修改型编码代理在设计上必须对这类攻击具备更强的韧性。

Abstract: Thompson's "Reflections on Trusting Trust" showed that a compiler can be poisoned to reinsert its own backdoor, so that even recompiling clean source reproduces the Trojan. Today, substantial coding work is done by AI coding agents -- and increasingly, those agents generate new versions of themselves. We reconsider Thompson's attack when the "compiler" is a self-modifying coding agent. Can an adversary supply poisoned benchmarks to the agent's self-evaluation and self-improvement process to induce future versions of the agent to write vulnerable code on clean, held-out tasks? We instantiate this attack against three recently proposed self-modifying coding agents: the Darwin Gödel Machine (with our experimental modifications), the Self-Improving Coding Agent, and Hyperagents (both substantively unmodified). We demonstrate successful proofs-of-concept: for example, with Hyperagents powered by Sonnet 4.5, our poisoned benchmark leads the agent to self-evolve instructions that disable HTTPS certificate validation on neutral URL-fetching tasks. From our experiments, we distill properties of the vulnerability, benchmark, model, and agent scaffolding that are sufficient to enable a benchmark poisoning attack. Moreover, we show that contamination often persists even when a poisoned agent is subsequently evolved against clean benchmarks. We discuss defensive directions and argue that self-modifying coding agents must be designed to be more resilient to such attacks.

</details>


### [289] [Evaluating the Impact of Personalization in Conversational Cybersecurity Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17839)
*Lea Duesterwald,Anika Jain,Shreya Kochar,Norman Sadeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究如何通过个性化策略提升LLM网络安全助手的回答效果，发现基于对话/交互历史的个性化最受用户青睐，且LLM自动评估与人工评估趋势一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户越来越多地使用大语言模型回答包括网络安全在内的各类问题，但用户常常不遵循安全建议，因此除了准确性外，回答的可理解性、可操作性和激励力同样重要，需要研究个性化策略能否提升这些效果。

Method: 提出并比较四种个性化策略，从静态用户画像到基于交互历史的个性化；使用1,045个真实网络安全问题语料，并进行涉及57名参与者、1,066个用户问题的7天部署；同时开展大规模自动化LLM评估与人工评估。

Result: 在感知有用性和遵循安全建议的可能性对比评分中，基于对话的个性化持续受到偏好；LLM自动评估的相对趋势与人工评估一致。

Conclusion: 行为驱动的个性化是LLM网络安全助手一个有前景的方向，也凸显了在研究个性化语言模型系统时结合LLM评估与人工评估的价值。

Abstract: Users increasingly turn to Large Language Models to answer a variety of questions, including cybersecurity questions. We study how personalization strategies can help improve the effectiveness of answers to questions asked to an LLM-based cybersecurity assistant. Beyond accuracy, we focus on the understandability, actionability and, most importantly, motivating power of answers, given how often users fail to follow cybersecurity recommendations. Specifically, we investigate four personalization strategies, ranging from static user profiles to interaction-history-based personalization, using a corpus of 1,045 real-world cybersecurity questions and a 7-day deployment involving 57 participants and 1,066 user questions. Across both a large-scale automated LLM-based evaluation and human evaluation, conversation-based personalization is consistently favored in comparative ratings of perceived helpfulness and likelihood of following security advice. Importantly, the relative trends observed in the LLM-based evaluation align with those obtained from human evaluation, suggesting that LLM-based evaluation can provide a scalable mechanism for comparing personalization strategies before costly user studies. These results indicate that behavior-driven personalization is a promising direction for LLM-powered cybersecurity assistants and highlight the value of combining LLM-based and human evaluation when studying personalized language-model systems.

</details>


### [290] [When AI Agents Meet MEV: Cross-Chain Arbitrage in the Agentic Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17897)
*Wei Ye,Jingyan Xu,Yuanhong Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究AI智能体作为搜索者的跨链套利问题，建模最优交易规模与多链路径选择，基于2.3万条Uniswap V3事件发现L2-L2跨链套利更可行，自适应算法优于基线11%，适度随机化可降低MEV暴露超50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨链套利研究通常假设搜索者是人类或普通机器人，而本文关注自主AI智能体作为搜索者这一新场景。同时，AI智能体既是套利提取者，也可能成为MEV（最大可提取价值）的目标，二者的双重身份及其在跨链桥延迟不确定性下的决策问题尚缺乏系统建模。

Method: 1）将智能体同时建模为套利提取者和MEV目标；2）在均值-方差效用下，考虑随机桥接延迟，推导风险规避智能体的最优交易规模；3）将多链路径选择形式化为基于信念的在线学习问题，并证明在Robbins-Monro调度下信念估计收敛；4）使用以太坊、Arbitrum和Base上23,000条Uniswap V3交易事件进行实证分析。

Result: 以太坊-Arbitrum价差在10秒分辨率下平均为0.044%，Arbitrum-Base价差平均为0.013%；通过CCTP，$10,000交易在63%的L2-L2窗口可清算，而L1-L2路线需要$50,000以上才具可比可行性。自适应路径选择算法平均优于标准基线11%；适度随机化在利润损失有限的情况下将MEV暴露降低超50%。

Conclusion: 自主AI智能体参与跨链套利时，L2-L2路径（如通过CCTP）比L1-L2路径更具经济可行性。采用信念加权在线学习和适度随机化的自适应策略，能够在保持合理利润的同时显著降低MEV暴露，为风险规避型跨链套利智能体提供了有效的决策框架。

Abstract: We study cross-chain arbitrage when autonomous AI agents, rather than humans or bots, are the searchers. We model agents as both arbitrage extractors and Maximal Extractable Value targets, derive the optimal trade size for a risk-averse agent under mean-variance utility with stochastic bridge delays, and formalize multi-chain path selection as a belief-weighted online learning problem whose belief estimates converge under a Robbins-Monro schedule. Using 23,000 Uniswap V3 swap events across Ethereum, Arbitrum, and Base, we find that Ethereum-Arbitrum price gaps average 0.044% at 10-second resolution and Arbitrum--Base gaps average 0.013%, so $10,000 trades clear in 63% of L2-L2 windows via CCTP while L1-L2 routes require $50,000 or more for comparable viability. Our adaptive path-selection algorithm outperforms standard baselines by 11% on average, and moderate randomization cuts MEV exposure by over 50% with only modest profit loss.

</details>


### [291] [Ghost-Filled Orders: Detecting and Testing Atomicity Violations in Non-Custodial Prediction Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17902)
*Zhiyang Chen,Fan Long,Zhendong Su*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文以 Polymarket 为案例，研究区块链预测市场中链下订单管理与链上结算之间的"原子性缺口"，即"幽灵成交订单"问题，量化其规模与财务影响，并验证其他三个同类市场同样存在此类攻击风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链预测市场采用链下订单簿撮合、链上结算的架构，虽实现了用户自主托管，却产生了原子性缺口：订单在链下接受或撮合时有效，但在链上结算执行前可能失效，即社区所称的"幽灵成交订单"。攻击者可利用该时间差，在观察到市场结果或价格变动后撤销不利订单，本文旨在系统研究这一问题。

Method: 1) 对 Polymarket 进行案例研究，分析链下订单接受与链上结算之间的延迟如何被攻击者利用；2) 使用 2025 年 8 月 12 日至 2026 年 5 月 22 日九个月期间、涉及 Polymarket 官方智能合约的 180 万笔回滚交易进行量化分析；3) 区分攻击者利润与市场及用户损失，并制定保守的度量规则以避免夸大影响；4) 提出一套可迁移的测试方法，应用于另外三个区块链预测市场。

Result: 在九个月窗口内识别出 180 万笔涉及 Polymarket 官方智能合约的回滚交易，量化了该"幽灵成交"行为的规模与财务影响，并区分了攻击者收益与市场、用户损失。对另外三个预测市场的测试显示，三者均易受同类攻击，其中一种设计可直接为攻击者带来利润。相关发现已报告给三个项目，其中一个项目在研究时已确认该问题。

Conclusion: 链下撮合与链上结算的分离结构在实现用户自主托管的同时引入了可被系统性利用的原子性缺口，"幽灵成交订单"并非 Polymarket 独有，而是该类架构的普遍风险，需在设计与度量层面加以应对。

Abstract: Blockchain based prediction markets combine offchain order management with onchain settlement. This architecture supports user controlled custody, since users keep funds in wallets or smart contracts while submitting signed orders to an offchain order book. However, it creates an atomicity gap. An order may be valid when accepted or matched offchain, but become invalid before the corresponding onchain settlement transaction is executed. This behavior, often called ghost filled orders by the community, can cause trades that appear filled offchain to fail onchain. This paper studies this atomicity gap through a case study of Polymarket. We show how the delay between offchain order acceptance and onchain settlement allows adversaries to invalidate unfavorable orders after observing market outcomes or price movements. We then quantify the scale and financial impact of this behavior over a nine-month period from August 12, 2025, to May 22, 2026, using 1.8 million reverted transactions involving Polymarket official smart contracts. Our analysis separates attacker profit from market and user loss, and develops conservative measurement rules to avoid overclaiming impact. We further develop a methodology for testing other blockchain based prediction markets and apply it to three additional markets. We find that all three are vulnerable to the same class of attack, and that one design enables direct attacker profit. We have reported the findings to all three projects; one project had acknowledged the issue at the time of the study.

</details>


### [292] [PentestChain: A Cost-Aware, MCP-Orchestrated Framework for Automated Penetration Testing with Free-Tier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18120)
*Rushabh Vipulkumar Patel,Dipo Dunsin,Mohammed Almaiah,Mohamed Chahine Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PentestChain是一个十阶段自动化渗透测试框架，通过确定性漏洞利用映射与成本感知AI级联（本地qwen2.5-7b→免费OpenRouter/Cerebras→规则回退）实现零付费API成本运行，并以MCP服务器暴露11个工具，同时给出威胁模型、四项缓解措施与可复现评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型（如GPT-4）驱动的渗透测试虽已验证可行，但每次交战的令牌成本高昂，使最需要自动化测试的中小型组织难以负担持续、自动化的测试。

Method: 提出PentestChain十阶段自动化渗透测试框架：将精心整理、确定性的漏洞利用映射与成本感知AI级联相结合——先用本地Ollama模型（qwen2.5-7b），再到免费层OpenRouter与Cerebras，并配有始终能产出输出的规则回退方案；通过带11个工具的MCP服务器暴露完整流水线。安全方面基于2025年MCP事件记录（mcp-remote的CVE-2025-6514 RCE漏洞、postmark-mcp供应链后门、工具投毒-撤回-跳线类攻击）构建四位置威胁模型并提出四项缓解措施。评测方面制定容器化可复现协议：AutoPenBench、Cybench子集、PentestGPT 182子任务基准，采用多次试验统计（每配置超10次试验、pass-at-k、非参数显著性检验与效应量），并直接在同测试床复现PentestGPT与PentestAgent基线。

Result: 在迄今测量的遗留目标上，框架检测到26个服务、富化了34个CVE（摘要在此处截断）。

Conclusion: 将每次交战的美元成本作为一等评测指标，证明由确定性主干保持在关键路径之外的7B参数本地模型可维持端到端运行且实测付费API成本为零；同时系统分析了MCP暴露攻击性引擎引入的攻击面并给出缓解措施，以及可复现的标准化评测协议。

Abstract: AI-driven penetration testing has been demonstrated with premium frontier models such as GPT-4, but the per-engagement token cost makes continuous, automated testing unaffordable for the smaller organisations that need it most. This paper presents PentestChain, a ten-phase automated penetration testing framework that couples a curated, deterministic exploit map with a cost-aware AI cascade-a local Ollama model (qwen2.5-7b) first, then free-tier OpenRouter and Cerebras, with a rule-based fallback that always produces output-and exposes the full pipeline through a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server with eleven tools. We make three contributions. First, we treat US-dollar cost per engagement as a measured, first-class evaluation metric and show that a 7B-parameter local model, kept off the critical path by a deterministic backbone, sustains end-to-end operation at zero measured paid-API cost. Second, we analyse the attack surface that an MCP-exposed offensive engine introduces, grounding a four-position threat model in the 2025 MCP incident record (the CVE-2025-6514 remote-code-execution flaw in mcp-remote, the postmark-mcp supply-chain backdoor, and the tool-poisoning-rug-pull-line-jumping class), and contribute four mitigations. Third, we specify a reproducible, containerised evalua-tion protocol aligned with the standardised testbeds now expected at top-tier venues-AutoPenBench, a Cybench subset, and the PentestGPT 182-sub-task benchmark-with multi-trial statistics (more than 10 trials per configuration, pass-at-k, non-parametric significance tests and effect sizes) and direct, same testbed reproduction of the PentestGPT and PentestAgent baselines rather than citation of their published numbers. On the legacy targets measured to date, the framework detected 26 services, enriched 34 CVEs, produced

</details>


### [293] ["Your Robot Was Trained on a Lie": Collision Mesh Poisoning Attacks on Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18122)
*Gengyang Xu,Dongwei Xiao,Yiteng Peng,Yanbo Dai,Ruochen Zhou,Shing-Chi Cheung,Xiaoyu Ji,Wenyuan Xu,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出碰撞网格投毒（CMP）攻击，利用仿真器中视觉网格与碰撞网格不一致的"视觉-碰撞间隙"，通过对3D资产供应链的碰撞网格投毒，使机器人在仿真中表现正常但真实世界部署时失效或产生安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略训练依赖仿真器，而仿真器中3D资产包含视觉网格与碰撞网格两套独立几何。为计算效率，碰撞网格通常被粗糙近似，与视觉网格存在合法且普遍的差异（视觉-碰撞间隙）。现有资产审核仅覆盖恶意软件、版权与格式合规，不检查视觉与碰撞一致性，因此该间隙构成新的攻击面，亟需研究其安全性影响。

Method: 提出碰撞网格投毒（CMP）攻击，这是首个通过3D资产供应链针对机器人操作实施的投毒攻击。攻击者仅修改3D资产的碰撞网格，保持视觉网格及其他组件不变。投毒资产经合法供应链渠道分发，策略在仿真中训练与评估表现正常，但在真实世界部署时降级、失败或引发物理安全风险。同时评估若干现有防御方法对CMP的有效性。

Result: 实验表明，现有多种防御方法均不足以抵御CMP攻击，说明该攻击具有现实可行性与隐蔽性。

Conclusion: 视觉-碰撞间隙是机器人仿真中一个被忽视却实际存在的攻击面，CMP攻击可绕过当前审核机制并经合法渠道传播。现有防御不足，凸显了开发新型防御手段的必要性。

Abstract: Learning-enabled robotic manipulation increasingly relies on robot simulators for policy training and evaluation before real-world deployment. Inside a simulator, a 3D asset contains two separate geometries: a visual mesh used for rendering and a collision mesh used for physical interaction. For computational efficiency, the collision mesh is deliberately a coarse approximation that need not have the same geometry as the visual mesh, a legitimate and pervasive discrepancy we call the Visual--Collision Gap (V--C Gap). We show that the V--C Gap opens a new and practical attack surface, and propose Collision Mesh Poisoning (CMP), the first poisoning attack against robotic manipulation delivered through the 3D asset supply chain. An attacker modifies only the collision mesh of a 3D asset, leaving the visual mesh and all other components unchanged. A policy trained and evaluated with the poisoned asset behaves normally throughout simulation, yet degrades, fails, or creates physical safety risks once deployed in the real world. Since current asset review practices cover malware, copyright, and format compliance, but not visual--collision consistency, poisoned assets can be distributed through legitimate supply chain channels. We evaluate several defenses and our results show that they are insufficient to defend against CMP, highlighting the need for new defenses.

</details>


### [294] [Bridging the Opacity: Evidence-Backed Cross-Chain Transaction Correspondence Reconstruction Across Heterogeneous Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18158)
*Dan Lin,Huan Xiao,Ziwei Li,Xiapu Luo,Jiachi Chen,Jiajing Wu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出证据驱动的跨链交易对应重建系统XSplicer，无需特权访问桥后端，在七种桥协议上达到92.5%全局恢复率，并成功应用于真实案件取证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨链桥实现互操作性但破坏了追踪非法资金的交易链条。第三方调查者通常无法访问桥后端维护的源-目的映射，对131座桥的调查显示仅16.79%提供完整公开追踪。现有方法依赖官方API、EVM特定假设或脆弱的时间启发式，难以跨异构账本追踪。

Method: XSplicer从公开协议文档和交易示例中提取统一语义规范，将其转化为轻量级解析器和验证器，并通过优先使用硬证据、仅在必要时使用软线索来关联源交易与目的交易。

Result: 在覆盖EVM、比特币和Solana的七种桥协议上评估，全局恢复率达92.5%，单协议最高98.61%。对抗噪声下硬证据验证器保持100%正确匹配，软线索匹配随歧义增加而退化。案例研究中，Multichain停运后恢复超1,900对历史交易，Bybit洗钱事件中识别754笔非法跨链转账，价值1.056亿美元。

Conclusion: 公开协议不变量可支持实用的跨链取证，无需特权桥映射。

Abstract: Cross-chain bridges enable interoperability, but they also break the transaction trails needed to trace illicit funds. Third-party investigators typically cannot access the source-to-destination mappings maintained by bridge backends, and our survey of 131 bridges finds that only 16.79% provide complete public tracking. Existing approaches depend on official APIs, EVM-specific assumptions, or fragile temporal heuristics, limiting their ability to trace transfers across heterogeneous ledgers. We present XSplicer, an evidence-driven system for reconstructing cross-chain transaction correspondence (xTCR) without privileged access to bridge backends. XSplicer derives unified semantic specifications from public protocol documentation and transaction examples, translates them into lightweight parsers and verifiers, and links source and destination transactions by prioritizing hard evidence and using soft clues only when necessary. We evaluate XSplicer on seven bridge protocols spanning EVM, Bitcoin, and Solana. XSplicer achieves 92.5% global recovery rate and up to 98.61% on individual protocols. Under adversarial noise, its hard-evidence verifier retains the correct match in 100% of tested cases, while soft-clue matching degrades as ambiguity increases. In two real-world case studies, XSplicer recovers more than 1,900 historical transaction pairs after Multichain ceased operations and identifies 754 illicit cross-chain transfers worth 105.6 million USD in the Bybit laundering incident. These results show that public protocol invariants can support practical cross-chain forensics without privileged bridge mappings.

</details>


### [295] [ISIA-AF: Orchestrating Reproducible Attacks and Multi-Source Data Collection for OT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18196)
*Stefan Manfred Haratzmüller,Thomas Rosenstatter,Olaf Saßnick,Dalibor Sain,Stefan Huber*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ISIA-AF，一个模块化攻击框架，用于在工业系统中编排可复现的攻击执行并自动化生成多源OT安全数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OT环境需要真实、可复现的安全数据集，但现有方法缺乏自动化、多源数据采集和足够的文档说明，难以复用。

Method: 从先前工作和利益相关者讨论中提炼功能与非功能需求，采用设计科学研究方法实现该框架。框架协调分布式攻击客户端，记录网络流量与运行数据，形成多源数据集。在ISIA测试床（含真实工业系统和模拟过程）上进行案例研究。

Result: 案例研究证明该框架支持集中控制、低通信开销，并能灵活部署于不同网段。

Conclusion: 该框架为生成可扩展、多源的OT安全数据集提供了实用基础，可支持入侵检测研究。

Abstract: Operational Technology (OT) environments require realistic, reproducible security datasets, yet existing approaches often lack automation, multi-source data capture, and sufficient documentation for reuse. This paper presents ISIA-AF, a modular attack framework for orchestrating reproducible attack execution and automated dataset generation on industrial systems. The framework coordinates distributed attack clients, records network traffic and operational data, ultimately leading to a multi-source dataset. We derive functional and non-functional requirements from prior work and stakeholder discussions, and realise the framework following a design science research approach. A case study on the ISIA testbed, comprising a real industrial system and a simulated process, demonstrates how the framework supports centralised control, low communication overhead, and flexible deployment across network segments. The result is a practical basis for generating extensible, multi-source OT security datasets for intrusion detection research.

</details>


### [296] [Measuring and Exploiting Implicit Trust in LLM Tool-Calling Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18217)
*Murali Ediga,Sudipta Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文揭示MCP（Model Context Protocol）中多输入通道共享单一上下文窗口且无权限隔离的安全隐患，提出跨通道分片注入攻击，可将完整恶意载荷拆分到2-3个通道，绕过单通道防御。在12个前沿模型、3个生产客户端、6种载荷上超15000次试验显示，完全抵抗单通道注入（0%合规）的模型在两通道分片下数据外泄率高达100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP使LLM能调用外部工具，但每次工具交互都通过多个输入通道（工具描述、工具结果、采样消息）向模型暴露攻击者可控文本，这些通道共享同一上下文窗口且缺乏权限隔离，形成未被探索的攻击面。现有防御与安全工具主要针对单通道注入，无法应对跨通道分片组合攻击。

Method: 1) 提出一个评估框架，通过不同通道发送多种载荷框架，测量任意LLM的"信任画像"；2) 基于该评估设计跨通道分片攻击，将看似无害的载荷分散到两到三个通道，使单个通道均不包含完整注入，但模型会将分片拼合为凭据外泄行为；3) 演示价值对齐式利用（工具声明用途本身需要攻击者所针对的数据）及通过VS Code的MCP实现注入持久指令的采样系统提示覆盖；4) 对7种第三方MCP安全工具与3种基于提示的防御进行测试。

Result: 覆盖12个前沿模型、3个生产客户端、6种载荷，共超过15000次试验。结果显示：完全抵抗单通道注入（0%合规）的模型在两通道分片下外泄敏感数据比例最高达100%（如GPT-4o、Llama 70B、Composer 2、Haiku 4.5）。7种第三方MCP安全工具均未能检测到分片载荷；3种基于提示的防御表现依赖具体模型，不具备通用性。此外成功演示了价值对齐式利用与VS Code MCP持久指令注入。

Conclusion: 跨通道分片攻击是一个此前未被探索的攻击面，能突破单通道下表现稳健的模型的防御。现有第三方MCP安全工具与提示式防御均无法有效应对，说明当前MCP生态在通道间缺乏权限隔离的设计存在系统性安全缺口，亟需针对跨通道组合威胁的防护机制。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) enables LLMs to invoke external tools, but every tool interaction exposes the model to attacker-controlled text through multiple input channels (tool descriptions, tool results, sampling messages) that share a single context window without privilege separation. In this paper, we present a framework to measure the trust profile of an arbitrary LLM based on a variety of payload framings sent through different channels. Following this assessment, we devise cross-channel fragmentation attacks that distribute seemingly benign payloads across two or three channels; no individual channel carries a complete injection, yet the LLM compiles the fragments into credential exfiltration. We evaluated our attacks across 12 frontier models, three production clients, and six payloads, totalling over 15,000 trials. Our evaluation reveals that cross-channel attacks are an unexplored attack surface: models that fully resist single-channel injection (0% compliance) exfiltrate sensitive data at up to 100% under two-channel fragmentation (e.g., GPT-4o, Llama 70B, Composer 2, Haiku 4.5). We further demonstrate value-aligned exploitation, where a tool's stated purpose requires the data the attacker targets, and a sampling system prompt override that injects persistent instructions via VS Code's MCP implementation. Finally, we evaluated our attacks against seven third-party MCP security tools and three prompt-based defenses. All tools failed to detect fragmented payloads, and prompt defenses proved model-specific rather than universal.

</details>


### [297] [Witness Encryption via Prime-Order Generic Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18275)
*Isaac M Hair,Amit Sahai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在经典通用群模型中无条件构造了针对NP的见证加密方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典通用群模型下无条件构造NP的见证加密，并推动同构MinRank近似硬度研究。

Method: 使用普通素数阶循环群，在经典通用群模型中构造见证加密；加密运行时间为poly(n)，解密为poly(n)且正确性误差为2^{-n^{Ω(1)}}；安全归约至同构MinRank近似问题。

Result: 无满足赋值时，任何最多进行n^{Θ(log n)}次群查询的通用敌手区分优势至多n^{-Θ(log n)}；并首次给出同构MinRank在随机多项式时间归约下超越常数因子的NP-hard近似结果，即便rank-one见证的右因子为布尔向量也能达到对数间隙。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了NP见证加密的无条件通用群模型构造，并建立了同构MinRank的对数间隙近似硬度。

Abstract: We unconditionally construct witness encryption for NP in the classical generic-group model, using an ordinary cyclic group of prime order. For SAT instances of size $n$, the encryption algorithm runs in time poly$(n)$, and any satisfying assignment can be used to decrypt in poly$(n)$ time with correctness error $2^{-n^{Ω(1)}}$. If no satisfying assignment exists, then every generic adversary making at most $n^{Θ(\log n)}$ group queries has distinguishing advantage at most $n^{-Θ(\log n)}$.
  Along the way, we prove the first superconstant-factor NP-hardness of approximation result for homogeneous MinRank under randomized polynomial-time reductions, achieving a logarithmic gap even when the rank-one witness has a Boolean right factor.

</details>


### [298] [Autonomy in Check: Governor-Mediated Adaptive Security at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18338)
*Ijaz Ahmad,Ijaz Ahmad,Flavio Esposito,Erkki Harjula*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"拆分控制"架构,将不可信规划器与内核执行之间的边界作为首要安全对象:规划器仅输出类型化安全意图,由确定性 governor 依据安全、资源、时间稳定性和比例性不变量进行准入判定,只有被许可的动作才绑定签名回执并编译为预装 eBPF map 更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络边缘的自适应安全日益依赖自动规划器(规则控制器、学习策略、LLM 辅助智能体)将观测转化为执行动作。一旦规划器能影响实时策略状态,仅靠语法有效性已不足:由不完整或被操纵观测产生的语义错误动作,会被无法判断任务上下文的执行基底忠实执行。因此需要把规划器输出与内核执行输入之间的边界当作首要安全对象。

Method: 提出拆分控制架构:不可信规划器发出类型化安全意图;确定性 governor 对每个意图检查安全、资源、时间稳定性和比例性四类不变量;仅被准入的动作绑定签名回执,并编译为预安装的 eBPF map 更新。论文形式化了该信任边界问题,定义了三类威胁,给出 governor 准入谓词,并实现端到端原型。

Result: 在连接大学 5G 测试网络的 Raspberry Pi 5 测试床上,针对规则式与 LLM 辅助规划器,governor 以微秒级开销完成意图的准入、拒绝与限界,且未破坏受保护流的规律性。

Conclusion: 贡献兼具概念性与实证性:自适应安全无需信任动作的作者,而需要一个判定动作是否可被准入的中介边界。

Abstract: Adaptive security at the network edge increasingly relies on automated planners, including rule-based controllers, learned policies, and LLM-assisted agents, that translate observations into enforcement actions. Once such a planner can influence live policy state, syntactic validity is not enough. A semantically wrong action, produced from incomplete or manipulated observations, can be faithfully executed by an enforcement substrate that cannot judge mission context. We address this problem by treating the boundary between planner output and kernel enforcement input as the primary security object. We propose a split-control architecture in which an untrusted planner emits typed security intents, a deterministic governor checks each intent against safety, resource, temporal-stability, and proportionality invariants, and only admitted actions are bound to signed receipts and compiled into pre-installed eBPF map updates. The paper formalizes this trust-boundary problem, defines three threat classes, develops the governor admission predicate, and reports an end-to-end prototype. Across rule-based and LLM-assisted planners on a Raspberry Pi 5 testbed connected to the university 5G Test Network, the governor admits, rejects, and bounds intents at microsecond cost without disrupting protected-flow regularity. The contribution is conceptual as much as empirical: adaptive security does not need to trust the author of an action. It needs a mediation boundary that decides whether the action is admissible.

</details>


### [299] [AIJon: Automated Generation of Annotations for Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18457)
*Jayakrishna Menon Vadayath,Hulin Wang,Moritz Schloegel,Jie Hu,Wil Gibbs,Tiffany Bao,Adam Doupé,Ruoyu "Fish" Wang,Yan Shoshitaishvili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文复现并扩展IJON实验，提出用LLM自动生成IJON风格注解构建AIJON，在Magma上评估发现注解式模糊测试并未严格优于AFL++，并分析了注解对能量分配等的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代模糊测试器以代码覆盖率为反馈，但会忽略那些不增加新代码路径却对目标程序有价值的输入。人工领域专家注解可提供额外反馈，但依赖专家、难以规模化。作者希望借助LLM自动生成注解，降低扩展成本，并在真实漏洞检测规模上验证注解式模糊测试的效果。

Method: 复现IJON实验并扩展到大规模真实漏洞检测；利用LLM自动生成IJON风格注解以缓解人力瓶颈；验证LLM注解的可用性；设计并实现AIJON系统，用LLM自动生成注解；在Magma基准上评估AIJON，并开展多组实验探究结果成因，包括注解对模糊测试能量分配的影响。

Result: LLM生成的注解性能可与人工注解相当；但在Magma基准上，基于注解的模糊测试并未严格优于AFL++。作者通过实验识别出该现象的原因，并总结出注解对模糊测试活动的若干关键影响，尤其是对模糊器能量分配的影响。

Conclusion: 注解式模糊测试并非在所有场景严格优于传统覆盖率引导方法；但LLM能够生成与人工质量相当的注解，为未来在大规模上研究注解影响打开了空间。

Abstract: Modern fuzzers use code coverage as feedback to guide their exploration which has proven to be an effective strategy for driving exploration. However, this strategy overlooks inputs that may be interesting to the target program even without uncovering new code paths. Fortunately, prior research has shown that annotations generated by human domain experts can provide additional feedback, guiding the fuzzer towards interesting parts of the program.
  In this paper, we replicate experiments presented in IJON and extend them to real-world vulnerability detection at scale. To mitigate the scalability challenge, imposed by the need for human domain expertise, we propose utilizing LLMs to automatically generate annotations. We demonstrate the applicability of LLMs for this purpose and observe that LLMs can generate annotations that perform comparably to human-generated annotations.
  Motivated by this finding, we design AIJON, a system that leverages LLMs to automatically generate IJON-style annotations. We evaluate AIJON on the Magma benchmark and surprisingly observe that annotation-based fuzzing does not perform strictly better than AFL++. We conduct several experiments to identify the cause of our results and identify key insights regarding the impact of annotations on fuzzing campaigns, including their effect on the energy distribution of the fuzzer. Notably, we observe that LLMs can generate annotations that achieve comparable results to human generated ones, thus opening the door for future research to perform further studies on the impact of annotations at scale.

</details>


### [300] [Detecting Logic Vulnerabilities Across the Contract and Device Layers of Blockchain-Enabled IoT With Multi-Agent Heterogeneous Graph Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18344)
*Minfeng Qi,Jialin Li,Tianqing Zhu,Lefeng Zhang,Zhe Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出跨层多智能体异构图注意力框架 MA-HGAT，统一建模区块链 IoT 系统中的合约、固件、设备集群与交易流，以四角色九关系模式支持多种逻辑漏洞检测任务，并可在资源受限网关上部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链赋能的 IoT 系统安全同时依赖链上合约逻辑与链下设备固件。两层中的逻辑缺陷会违反同一系统不变量（如未授权访问、状态篡改、特权操作失控）。现有方法多聚焦单层或单一工件，依赖预定义漏洞模式、仿真保真度或同构表示，掩盖了安全相关组件角色，且缺乏可统一支持多种安全任务并在资源受限网关上部署的架构。

Method: 将 MA-HGAT 扩展为跨层多智能体异构图注意力框架：以统一的四角色、九关系模式建模合约、固件工件、设备集群与交易流；角色对齐的智能体通过交叉注意力交换异构证据；设置图级、链路级与节点级预测头以支持多种检测任务；采用基于角色的网关—云端划分，实现轻量级边缘推理。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验数据或量化结果。框架在功能上支持多种检测任务（图级、链路级、节点级），并通过角色划分支持在资源受限网关上的轻量推理。

Conclusion: MA-HGAT 为区块链 IoT 系统提供统一且可部署的框架，可跨合约层与设备层检测逻辑漏洞。

Abstract: Blockchain-enabled Internet of Things (IoT) systems integrate smart contracts with embedded devices to support decentralized device management and access control. Their security therefore depends jointly on the logic of on-chain contracts and off-chain device firmware. Logic flaws in either layer can violate the same system invariants, such as unauthorized access, improper state changes, or unguarded privileged operations. Existing approaches rely on contract analysis, firmware analysis, and graph-based vulnerability detection. However, these methods typically focus on a single layer or artifact and often depend on predefined vulnerability patterns, emulation fidelity, or homogeneous representations that obscure security-relevant component roles. They also lack a unified architecture that supports different security tasks while remaining deployable on resource-constrained gateways. To address these limitations, we extend MA-HGAT into a cross-layer multi-agent heterogeneous graph attention framework that models contracts, firmware artifacts, device fleets, and transaction streams with a unified four-role, nine-relation schema. Role-aligned agents exchange heterogeneous evidence through cross-attention, while graph-, link-, and node-level heads support multiple detection tasks and a role-based gateway--cloud partition enables lightweight edge inference. MA-HGAT thus provides a unified and deployable framework for detecting logic vulnerabilities across the contract and device layers of blockchain-enabled IoT systems.

</details>


### [301] [The Verifiable Action Card: Trustworthy Human-in-the-Loop Control for Secure Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18411)
*Hasnain Irshad,Anam Mughees,Neelam Mughees,Abdullah Mughees,Imtiaz Ali Soomro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出可验证行动卡片（VAC），一种从真实待执行动作重建审批信息并在受信浏览器界面外带渲染的架构级防御，可将多种攻击成功率从68%-100%降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体浏览器在用户已认证会话下执行安全敏感操作，间接提示注入与欺骗性确认界面直接威胁动作完整性。当审批提示本身可能被不受信页面内容或模型生成文本影响时，现有的人类在环（HITL）防护不足。因此需要一种不依赖提示层防御的架构级保护。

Method: VAC 从真实待执行浏览器动作与可信意图溯源重建审批信息，在受信浏览器 chrome 中外带渲染，并将审批绑定到派发时重新验证的确切动作。其包含五项技术：溯源围栏、真实动作描述符、默认拒绝确认、溯源感知风险门控、执行绑定。作者在完整智能体浏览器中实现 VAC 并在 24 场景基准上评测。

Result: 基准涵盖混淆代理攻击、Lies-in-the-Loop 对话框伪造、间接提示注入、自适应动作替换、溯源规避与合法任务。在所评测的多个 LLM 上，无 VAC 时攻击成功率为68%-100%；VAC 将每个模型的攻击成功率降至0%，同时合法任务完成率78%，误拦截率0%。

Conclusion: 将审批建立在真正将要执行的动作之上，可提供架构级保护，抵御提示层防御与传统 HITL 确认无法可靠阻止的安全失效。

Abstract: Agentic browsers can execute security-sensitive actions under a user's authenticated session, making indirect prompt injection and deceptive confirmation interfaces a direct threat to action integrity. Existing human-in-the-loop (HITL) safeguards are insufficient when the approval prompt itself can be influenced by untrusted page content or model-generated text. We present the \emph{Verifiable Action Card} (VAC), an architectural defence that reconstructs approval information from the ground-truth pending browser action and trusted intent provenance, renders it out-of-band in the trusted browser chrome, and binds approval to the exact action re-verified at dispatch. VAC combines provenance fencing, a ground-truth action descriptor, default-deny confirmation, provenance-aware risk gating, and execution binding. We implement VAC in a complete agentic browser and evaluate it on a 24-scenario benchmark covering confused-deputy attacks, Lies-in-the-Loop dialog forging, indirect prompt injection, adaptive action substitution, provenance evasion, and legitimate tasks. Across the evaluated LLMs, attack success without VAC ranges from $68\%$ to $100\%$, whereas VAC reduces attack success to $0\%$ on every model, with $78\%$ legitimate-task completion and a $0\%$ false-block rate. These results show that grounding approval in the action that will actually execute provides architectural protection against security failures that prompt-level defences and conventional HITL confirmation cannot reliably prevent.

</details>


### [302] [SEEK: Secure and Efficient Encrypted Keyword Search For Privacy-Preserving Messaging Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18459)
*Soumyadyuti Ghosh,Michail Maniatakos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SEEK 是一种结合同态加密与安全两方计算的高效加密关键词搜索协议，用于隐私保护消息通信，在隐藏关键词及消息内容的前提下检测加密消息中的关键词，并实现端到端应用原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密通信保护了用户敏感数据，但也可能被用于有害或非法信息交换，因此需要在检测危险消息与保护终端用户隐私之间取得平衡。现有方案在加密关键词搜索场景下存在开销大、效率低的问题，亟需一种兼顾隐私与效率的实用协议。

Method: SEEK 将消息切分为具有最小充分重叠的密文片段，利用加密关键词陷门进行同态关联；针对长消息降低发送端加密与上传开销。支持 ASCII 大小写不敏感匹配，每个片段仅需一个固定大小的加密陷门和一次同态乘法。随后调用基于 2PC 的选择性解码、盲化零测试与安全聚合，仅泄露关键词是否存在这一比特。

Result: 相比最强基线，长消息发送端加密与上传开销最多降低两个数量级；关联计算速度最多提升 5.47 倍；在大小写变化导致精确匹配失败的场景下仍达到 100% 准确率，且无需额外陷门或在线通信。原型在每周消息历史上的在线计算时间为每次搜索 1.92 秒。

Conclusion: SEEK 在隐藏关键词、关键词长度、消息内容、匹配次数与位置的同时，实现了高效、准确的加密关键词搜索，并通过 Web 与跨平台移动应用原型验证了其实际可行性与效率。

Abstract: Encrypted communication protects sensitive user data but can facilitate harmful or unlawful exchanges, creating a trade-off between detecting dangerous messages and preserving end-user privacy. To address this, we propose SEEK, a practical and efficient encrypted keyword-search protocol for privacy-preserving messaging that combines homomorphic encryption with secure two-party computation (2PC). SEEK first partitions messages into ciphertext fragments with the minimum sufficient overlap, then homomorphically correlates them using encrypted keyword trapdoors. For long messages, this design can reduce sender-side encryption and upload overhead by up to two orders of magnitude over state-of-the-art baselines. It supports ASCII case-insensitive matching with one fixed-size encrypted trapdoor and one homomorphic multiplication per fragment, yielding up to 5.47x faster correlation computation than the strongest fragmentation-based baselines. SEEK then invokes 2PC-based selected decoding, blinded zero testing, and secure aggregation, revealing only the keyword presence-or-absence bit while hiding the keyword, its length, message contents, match counts, and locations. SEEK achieves 100% accuracy under case variations that result in exact-matching failures, without requiring additional trapdoors or online communication. We further realize SEEK as an end-to-end web and cross-platform mobile application. Prototype evaluation on a weekly messaging history yields an online computation time of 1.92 s per search, demonstrating the practical feasibility and efficiency of SEEK.

</details>


### [303] [A Global Readiness and Sovereignty Capability Model for Post-Quantum Cryptography Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18477)
*Mohamed Aly Bouke*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RSCM（准备度-主权能力模型），将后量子密码准备度与密码主权统一量化，对57个密码行为体评估后发现20国通过"制造者"门槛（15个全栈、5个研究型），25国处于依赖状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码依赖问题由来已久，但向后量子密码（PQC）迁移提供了重塑密码依赖与主权的罕见窗口期，因为当前选定的算法、实现、硬件与标准将锁定未来数十年的依赖或主权格局。现有成熟度模型只衡量"准备度"，而主权文献只做定义而不做测量，缺乏可操作的国家层面测量工具。

Method: 提出RSCM国家测量模型，将主权分解为三个独立构念：本土密码能力、本土后量子控制权、外部依赖；并设置"门槛"机制，要求至少在设计、实现或验证某一核心层面具备经制度性维持的自主创造能力，才可认证为后量子制造者。对57个有文献记载的密码行为体依据公开证据进行编码，并采用独立第二编码者、合理状态自助法（bootstrap）及收敛效度检验来验证编码。

Result: 20国通过门槛：15国为全栈制造者，5国为研究型制造者；11国具备强一般能力但无后量子控制权；1国为准备就绪的采纳者；25国处于依赖状态。门槛单元格加权一致性较高，二次加权kappa为0.71，制造者分类在核心部分稳定但在阈值附近存在不确定性。准备度与独立网络指数秩相关高达0.70，而后量子创造与承诺指数无显著相关（秩相关仅0.22），表明控制权与准备度可分离。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于提出RSCM框架、基于证据分级的评估方法，以及构建本土量子安全能力的政策方向，为各国在PQC迁移窗口期实现密码主权提供可测量、可操作的工具。

Abstract: Cryptographic dependence predates the quantum era, but the migration to post-quantum cryptography (PQC) opens a rare window to reshape it, because the algorithms, implementations, hardware, and standards adopted now can lock in dependence or sovereignty for decades. This paper introduces the Readiness-Sovereignty Capability Model (RSCM), a national measurement model that operationalizes PQC readiness together with cryptographic sovereignty, which current maturity models score only as readiness and the sovereignty literature defines without measuring. RSCM decomposes sovereignty into three distinct constructs, indigenous cryptographic capacity, indigenous post-quantum control, and external dependency, and certifies a post-quantum maker only through a gate requiring demonstrated, institutionally sustained creation in at least one core layer, whether design, implementation, or validation. Applying it to fifty-seven documented cryptographic actors coded from cited public evidence, and testing that coding with an independent second coder, a plausible-state bootstrap, and convergent-validity checks, we find that twenty countries clear the gate, fifteen as full-stack makers and five as research makers, eleven hold strong general capacity without post-quantum control, one is a ready adopter, and twenty-five are dependent. The gate cells show substantial weighted agreement, a quadratic-weighted kappa of 0.71, and the maker classification is stable in its core though uncertain at the threshold. Readiness tracks independent cyber indices at rank correlations up to 0.70, while post-quantum creation shows no significant correlation with the commitment index, a rank correlation of only 0.22 that separates control from readiness. The paper contributes the framework, the evidence-graded assessment, and policy directions for building indigenous quantum-safe capacity.

</details>


### [304] [MiST: Mid-Training LLMs for Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18496)
*Oded Ovadia,Elad Ben Zaken,Elad Guttman,Orly Moreno Kadosh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MiST（Mid-trained Security Transformer），包含8B和32B模型，通过在通用预训练与网络安全训练之间引入"中间训练"阶段，并利用专家审核的种子语料合成高质量领域数据，在公开网络安全基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全领域兼具高风险分析与复杂技术语言，对LLM而言是极具影响力但充满挑战的应用场景。传统做法是对大量原始领域文本进行持续预训练，但这种方式可能效率低、数据质量难控。作者希望找到一种更高效的领域适应方式。

Method: 提出MiST套件（8B与32B）。核心方法是"中间训练"（mid-training），作为通用预训练与网络安全训练之间的过渡适应阶段。不采用对海量原始领域文本的持续预训练，而是整理一个紧凑、经专家审核的种子语料库，并将其转化为高质量的领域特定合成训练数据。

Result: 相比对应的Qwen基线，MiST最终检查点在网络安全准确率上分别提升：8B模型+13.1个百分点（相对提升+27.0%），32B模型+8.6个百分点（相对提升+15.8%）。消融实验显示，这些性能增益来自中间训练和有监督微调阶段，通过合成数据生成流程的组合实现。此外，MiST为下游任务特定的微调适应和强化学习提供了更强的初始化。

Conclusion: 中间训练结合专家审核种子语料与合成数据生成，是提升LLM网络安全能力的有效途径，同时为下游微调和强化学习提供更优起点。该范式可推广至其他专业领域。

Abstract: Cybersecurity combines high-stakes analysis with complex technical language, making it an impactful and challenging domain for LLMs. We present MiST (Mid-trained Security Transformer), a suite of 8B and 32B models that achieve strong performance on public cybersecurity benchmarks. We use mid-training as an intermediate adaptation stage between general pre-training and cybersecurity training. Rather than performing continual pre-training over large volumes of raw domain text, we curate a compact, expert-vetted seed corpus, and transform it into high-quality domain-specific synthetic training data. The final MiST checkpoints improve mean cybersecurity accuracy by +13.1 and +8.6 absolute percentage points over the corresponding Qwen baselines for 8B and 32B, respectively, corresponding to relative gains of +27.0% and +15.8%. Ablation results further show that these cybersecurity gains arise in the mid-training and supervised fine-tuning stages through a combination of the synthetic data generation flows. Furthermore, we show that MiST provides a stronger initialization for downstream task-specific fine-tuning adaptation and reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [305] [Robot Visions: Breaking reCAPTCHA at Zero Cost and Zero Shot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18518)
*Suphannee Sivakorn,Samantha Gottlieb*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 免费的本地开源模型（CLIP、OWLv2）可零样本攻破Google reCAPTCHA，端到端会话成功率92.6%，且非技术对手仅凭自然语言指令即可使用消费级AI助手完成攻击，视觉挑战式CAPTCHA或已走到寿命终点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Google reCAPTCHA长期作为抵御自动化攻击（撞库、批量注册、表单滥用）的关键防线，但已有研究表明商用云端视觉语言模型（VLM）可破解视觉挑战，只是每次尝试成本不低。本文的动机在于验证更低门槛的威胁：无需付费、无需API、无需训练的本地开源模型是否也能攻破reCAPTCHA，从而重新评估其实际安全性。

Method: 1）对reCAPTCHA开展系统研究，提出挑战类型分类法：Type A（独立图像瓦片，含静态与动态子类型）与Type B（单图划分为4x4网格），各自需要不同的求解策略。2）设计完全基于开源本地模型的零样本、零成本求解器：CLIP用于Type A（单挑战准确率58%），OWLv2用于Type B（43.5%），无需模型训练、无需API访问。3）构建端到端自动化求解器并在500个真实reCAPTCHA会话上评估。4）演示非技术对手仅通过向商用AI助手输入自然语言指令即可完成破解。

Result: 端到端自动化求解器在500个真实reCAPTCHA会话上达到92.6%的单会话成功率；Type A上CLIP达58%单挑战准确率，Type B上OWLv2达43.5%；非技术对手借助自然语言指令配合消费级AI助手即可击败reCAPTCHA，将实际攻击者的技能门槛降至接近零。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，免费本地模型即可有效攻破Google reCAPTCHA，攻击者技能门槛被大幅拉低，挑战式CAPTCHA的威胁模型已发生根本改变。尽管reCAPTCHA日益依赖基于声誉的验证，但作为安全兜底的视觉挑战回退机制反而成为防御链中最薄弱的一环，暗示基于视觉挑战的CAPTCHA可能已走到其有效生命周期的终点。

Abstract: Google reCAPTCHA is the most widely deployed visual CAPTCHA service, protecting hundreds of thousands of websites from automated bots. It serves as a critical line of defense against automated attacks, including credential stuffing, bulk account creation, and automated form abuse. It has proven largely effective since its introduction in 2007. However, the rise of accessible AI now threatens its efficacy. Prior work has demonstrated that commercial cloud-based vision-language models (VLMs) can solve visual CAPTCHA challenges, but at non-trivial monetary cost per attempt. In this paper, we show that free and locally-run models can break Google reCAPTCHA. We conduct a comprehensive study of reCAPTCHA and present a taxonomy of its challenge types: Type A (independent image tiles, with static and dynamic sub-variants) and Type B (a single image partitioned into a 4x4 grid), each demanding a distinct solving strategy.
  We design zero-shot, no-cost solvers built entirely on open-source local models, specifically CLIP (58% per-challenge accuracy on Type A) and OWLv2 (43.5% on Type B), requiring no model training and no API access. Our end-to-end automated solver achieves a 92.6% per-session success rate across 500 real-world reCAPTCHA sessions. We further demonstrate that reCAPTCHA can be defeated by a non-technical adversary, using only natural-language instructions to a commodity AI assistant. This collapses the practical attacker skill floor to near zero and fundamentally changes the threat model for challenge-based CAPTCHAs. Although reCAPTCHA increasingly favors reputation-based verification, visual challenge-based fallback persists as a safety net that, paradoxically, has become the weakest link in the defense chain, suggesting that challenge-based visual CAPTCHAs may have reached the end of their useful life.

</details>


### [306] [The Illusion of Local Privacy: Confidentiality Boundary Failures in Consumer LLM Serving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18526)
*Youssef Hamdi Zafan Ibrahim,Muhammad Ikram,Mohammed Khalaf Salama*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LLAnalyzer测量框架，系统评估本地LLM推理中提示词机密性的四个失效边界，发现本地部署并不足以保证提示词保密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们普遍认为本地运行大语言模型比云端推理更私密，因为用户提示词留在设备上。作者质疑"本地推理"本身是否足以保证提示词机密性，认为还取决于服务软件在推理前、中、后如何处理提示词数据。

Method: 提出LLAnalyzer测量框架，分别测试提示词机密性可能失效的四个边界：模型加载、运行时内存、包装层持久化、服务接口，并将观察到的失效追溯到具体软件组件。在四个开源模型系列和两个消费级部署平台上进行评估，并进行AFL++模糊测试与受控攻击实验。

Result: 24小时AFL++测试超1200万次执行，未发现解析器崩溃或恶意GGUF成功加载；运行时内存中多种明文表示残留在分配器管理内存中，推理后可恢复提示词，清理可减少但无法消除残留；消费级包装层通过明文持久化延长提示词生命周期；发现llama.cpp中此前未记录的授权缺陷，一个已认证客户端可恢复另一租户的会话状态，200/200次受控试验成功；共享提示前缀缓存在WAN条件下仍暴露可区分的远程时序预言机。

Conclusion: 本地LLM系统需要在提示词生命周期、持久化存储和租户隔离方面提供明确的安全保证，仅保持推理本地化不足以保证提示词机密性。

Abstract: Running large language models (LLMs) locally is often considered more private than cloud-hosted inference because user prompts remain on the device. We ask whether keeping inference local is, by itself, sufficient to keep those prompts confidential. Our results show that it is not: prompt confidentiality also depends on how the surrounding serving software handles prompt data before, during, and after inference. We examine four boundaries at which prompt confidentiality can fail in consumer local-LLM serving systems: model loading, runtime memory, wrapper-level persistence, and the serving interface. To study these boundaries, we develop LLAnalyzer, a measurement framework that tests each boundary separately and traces observed failures to the responsible software component. Applying LLAnalyzer to four open-weight model families and two consumer deployment platforms, we find markedly different behaviour across boundaries. In a 24-hour AFL++ campaign with more than 12 million executions, we observe no parser crashes or successful malformed GGUF loads within the explored state space. Runtime memory tells a different story: we recover prompts after inference because multiple plaintext representations survive in allocator-managed memory, and sanitisation reduces this residue without eliminating it. We also find that consumer wrappers can extend prompt lifetime through plaintext persistence. At the serving boundary, we uncover a previously undocumented authorization flaw in llama.cpp that allows one authenticated client to restore another tenant's saved conversation state; the attack succeeds in 200/200 controlled trials. Separately, shared prompt-prefix caching exposes a remote timing oracle that remains distinguishable under WAN conditions. We argue that local LLM systems need explicit guarantees for prompt lifetime, persistent storage, and tenant isolation.

</details>


### [307] [A Security Risk Assessment Framework for AI-Powered Development Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18658)
*Salem AlJanah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出安全风险评估框架SRF，结合威胁建模、安全分析与基于漏洞严重性的定量风险评价，评估AI生成代码的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI开发工具已广泛用于代码生成，现有研究多聚焦漏洞检测而非风险评估，缺少系统化的风险量化手段，因此需要构建面向AI生成代码的安全风险评估框架。

Method: SRF整合威胁建模、安全分析和基于漏洞严重性的定量风险评估；应用于一组安全相关编程任务，使用Bandit与Semgrep对多个AI开发工具生成的代码进行分析。

Result: 所有被评估工具生成的代码均可能引入安全漏洞；风险水平随任务类型变化，输入处理与文件处理任务风险较高，较简单任务风险较低；工具间差异存在但小于任务类别间差异。

Conclusion: SRF支持对AI生成代码进行可复现的安全评估，为评估其安全影响提供了实用框架。

Abstract: AI-powered development tools are now widely used to generate code and assist developers with routine programming tasks. Although existing work has identified vulnerabilities in AI-generated code, security-oriented work is often focused on vulnerability detection rather than risk assessment. To address this gap, this paper presents a Security Risk Assessment Framework (SRF) to evaluate the security risks of AI-generated code. SRF combines threat modeling, security analysis, and a quantitative risk evaluation approach based on vulnerability criticality. The framework is applied to a set of security-relevant programming tasks, where code generated by multiple AI-powered development tools is analyzed using Bandit and Semgrep. The results show that AI-generated code can introduce security vulnerabilities across all evaluated tools. They also show that risk levels vary by task type, as input processing and file handling tasks showed higher risk, while simpler tasks remained low-risk. Differences between tools exist but are smaller than differences across task categories. Overall, SRF enables reproducible evaluation of AI-generated code and provides a practical framework for assessing its security implications.

</details>


### [308] [CaMeLoT: CaMeL orchestrated with Temporal logic for static verification and liveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18674)
*Elia Nikolaou,Magnus Wiik Eckhoff,Robert Flood,Gudmund Grov,Vasileios Mavroeidis,David Aspinall*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 CaMeLoT，在 LLM 智能体执行计划前引入静态验证层，将计划转化为有限状态迁移系统并用 nuXmv 检查 CTL 时序策略，实现执行前的安全验证并支持反例修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体会生成并执行多步计划，调用可访问私有数据或执行命令的外部工具。此时安全性是整个计划执行过程的属性，而非单个步骤的属性，因此需要在执行前对计划进行验证。

Method: CaMeLoT 作为 CaMeL 的补充，在调用任何工具前加入静态验证层。它将生成的计划翻译为有限状态迁移系统，标注工具调用、来源与污点信息，并使用 nuXmv 模型检查器针对以 CTL 表达的时序策略进行验证。验证失败时模型检查器返回反例，用于反馈给智能体修复计划。

Result: 在来自 AgentDojo 基准、SOC 工作流和提示提取实验的策略上进行评估，结果表明 CaMeLoT 能在执行前验证广泛类别的时序属性，同时保持 CaMeL 可运行时检查的覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 由于验证发生在执行前，不安全计划会被直接拒绝，无需消耗 LLM 调用或工具调用，节省 token 和回滚、拆除临时沙箱的成本，并提供反例反馈以修复计划。

Abstract: LLM-based agents generate and execute multi-step plans that invoke external tools which can access private data or execute commands. In this setting, security is a property of the entire execution that a plan creates, not just any single step. The plan itself is a critical artefact that captures the tool calls, control flow, and data dependencies. We present CaMeLoT, a complement to CaMeL, an existing defence against prompt injection in tool-using LLM agents. CaMeLoT extends CaMeL by adding a static verification layer that checks an agent's plan before any tool is invoked. CaMeLoT translates a generated plan into a finite-state transition system, labels it with tool calls, provenance and taint information, and checks it against temporal policies expressed in CTL using the nuXmv model checker. Because verification happens before execution, unsafe plans are rejected without using LLM calls or tool calls, saving tokens that runtime could have cost, as well as the need to unwind changes or teardown temporary sandboxes. When a verification fails, the model checker returns a counterexample to give feedback to the agent to repair the plan. We evaluate CaMeLoT on policies derived from the AgentDojo benchmark, SOC workflows, and prompt-extraction experiments, showing that it verifies a broad class of temporal properties before execution while preserving CaMeL's runtime-checkable coverage.

</details>


### [309] [Echo: Learning-based Matching Decompilation using Trusted Back Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18706)
*Jun Bi,Xiangxin Fang,Aarsh Chaube,José Wesley De Souza Magalhães,Rodrigo C. O. Rocha,Michael O'Boyle*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Echo，一个基于可信反向翻译的匹配式反编译系统，通过将编译作为迭代搜索的反馈信号，显著提升在优化二进制和未知编译配置下的精确匹配率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经反编译虽能恢复可读可重编译源码，但其预测难以信任；已有匹配式反编译要求重编译汇编与目标完全一致，但在优化二进制与未知编译配置下难以实现精确匹配。

Method: Echo先用领域特定模型生成候选程序与编译配置，重编译后度量汇编级相似度并合成有潜力的代码-配置对；对残余不匹配，依次使用规则化重写、神经精修和基于推理的精修进行逐步修复。

Result: 在函数级基准和Mirai恶意软件二进制上评估，Echo相较最强基线平均产生2.43倍更多精确匹配，并达到与真值源码最高的结构相似度；在Mirai上匹配函数数量分别是GPT-5.6和Codex的2.75倍和7.4倍。

Conclusion: 将编译同时用于验证与可信反馈来引导迭代搜索，可有效提升匹配式反编译在优化二进制场景下的精确匹配能力与源码结构还原质量。

Abstract: Neural decompilers can recover readable and recompilable source code from binaries, but their predictions remain difficult to trust. Matching decompilation addresses this problem by searching for source code whose recompiled assembly exactly matches the target, providing stronger evidence of correctness. However, exact matching remains challenging for optimized binaries under unknown compilation configurations.
  We present Echo, a matching decompilation system based on trusted back-translation. Our key insight is to use compilation not only for verification, but also as trusted feedback to guide iterative search. Echo first uses a domain-specific model to generate candidate programs and compilation configurations. It recompiles these candidates, measures assembly-level similarity, and synthesizes promising code-configuration pairs. Remaining mismatches are then progressively repaired using rule-based rewriting, neural refinement, and reasoning-based refinement.
  We evaluate Echo on function-level benchmarks and the Mirai malware binary. Compared with the strongest baseline, Echo produces 2.43x more exact matches on average and achieves the highest structural similarity to ground-truth source code. On Mirai, Echo matches 2.75x and 7.4x as many functions as GPT-5.6 and Codex, respectively.

</details>


### [310] [Normal Alignment: Improved Cryptanalytic Sign Recovery on Hard-Label Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18751)
*Shi Tang,Zirui Chen,Yongjia Su,Zhengchao Gao,Lingyue Qin,Xiaoyang Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Normal Alignment，一种针对硬标签（S1）深度神经网络的统计符号恢复方法，通过相邻决策面法向的期望长度差异进行法向-签名对齐，提升投票准确率并将错误预测推向低置信度，进而结合 eSOE 实现精确多项式时间的完整符号恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carlini 等人（EUROCRYPT 2025）在硬标签 DNN 的密码分析提取上取得突破，实现了多项式时间的签名与符号恢复。但其符号恢复方法（Future Toggle）相对随机猜测仅有微弱优势，在深层会产生高置信度的错误符号预测，从而触发代价高昂的指数级枚举。因此需要一种更准确、更高效的符号恢复方法。

Method: 提出 Normal Alignment：利用对偶点处相邻决策面投影法向之间的期望长度差异，通过法向-签名对齐来推断神经元符号。该方法具有更高的投票准确率，并能将错误预测推至低置信度排名。进一步将其与硬标签 SOE 扩展结合，形成 eSOE + Alignment 组合方法。

Result: 实验表明该方法（尤其对深层）有效。对于 CIFAR-10（192-64×8-10）和 MNIST（64-96×3-32-10）模型，本方法可在多项式时间内完整恢复符号；相比之下 Carlini 等人的方法分别需要涉及 2^52 或 2^82 次符号猜测的指数级枚举。

Conclusion: Normal Alignment 消除了繁重的枚举开销，结合 eSOE 实现了精确多项式时间的完整符号恢复，显著优于现有符号恢复方法。

Abstract: At EUROCRYPT 2025, Carlini et al. proposed a breakthrough in the cryptanalytic extraction on hard-label (S1) deep neural networks (DNNs), demonstrating polynomial-time signature and sign recovery. However, Carlini et al.'s sign-recovery method (which we call Future Toggle) suffers only a marginal advantage over random guessing, producing high-confidence wrong sign predictions in deeper layers. Such errors trigger expensive exponential-time enumeration.
  This work presents Normal Alignment, a novel statistical sign-recovery approach for S1 DNNs. Drawing on the expected length difference between projected normals of adjacent decision facets at dual points, our method infers neuron signs via normal-signature alignment. It delivers higher voting accuracy and pushes erroneous predictions to low-confidence ranks, which further enables a more efficient combined method, eSOE + Alignment, by combining Normal Alignment with the hard-label SOE extension. This combined strategy removes heavy enumeration overhead and realizes exact polynomial-time full sign recovery.
  Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, especially for deep layers. For example, with our method, the signs for CIFAR-10 (architecture 192-64$\times$8-10) and MNIST (architecture 64-96$\times$3-32-10) models can be fully recovered in polynomial time; in contrast, Carlini et al.'s sign-recovery method would require exponential-time enumerations involving $2^{52}$ or $2^{82}$ guesses of the signs, respectively.

</details>


### [311] [s-MDM: Generative Virtualization of Multi-Device Hardware Variations for Portable DL-SCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18783)
*Niloufar Sayadi,Marten van Dijk,Chenglu Jin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出s-MDM，一种无需目标设备真实轨迹的零样本生成框架，通过结构化cVAE、Walsh-Hadamard泄漏锚点、连续风格调制与解耦的泄漏-风格-域判别器合成虚拟源设备画像，以提升深度学习侧信道分析（DL-SCA）的跨设备可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DL-SCA在未见硬件上因PCB布线差异、硅工艺波动和测量噪声漂移，常出现灾难性性能退化，跨设备可移植性差，且现有物理MDM方法依赖真实目标轨迹、成本高。

Method: 提出s-MDM零目标轨迹生成框架：结合结构化cVAE生成器、Walsh-Hadamard泄漏锚点、连续风格调制，以及解耦的泄漏-风格-域判别器，离线合成虚拟源设备画像。

Result: 在AES_PTv2 32位侧信道轨迹上评测，s-MDM刻画了明确的操作边界：在相同电气克隆设备D4上物理MDM仍占优；但在布局/采集偏移的Pinata目标上，物理基线不稳定或失配，而s-MDM保持稳定低密钥排名。

Conclusion: s-MDM在无目标轨迹条件下，可在布局与采集偏移的目标设备上实现稳定跨设备迁移，弥补物理MDM在异构硬件场景下的不足。

Abstract: Deep Learning-based Side-Channel Analysis (DL-SCA) frequently suffers from catastrophic performance degradation across unseen hardware due to printed circuit board routing differences, silicon process variations, and measurement noise shifts. This poster presents the Synthetic Multiple Device Model (s-MDM), a zero-target-trace generative framework designed to improve cross-device portability. s-MDM combines a structured cVAE generator, a Walsh-Hadamard leakage anchor, continuous style modulation, and decoupled leakage-style--domain critics to synthesize virtual source-device profiles offline. Benchmarked on 32-bit side-channel traces (AES_PTv2), s-MDM maps a precise operational boundary: while physical MDM remains superior on identical electrical clones (D4), s-MDM achieves consistently low key rank on the layout/acquisition-shifted Pinata target, where physical baselines are unstable or misaligned.

</details>


### [312] [Differential Trust: Dynamic Multi-Authority Anonymous Credentials with Epoch-Weighted Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18811)
*Chen Li,Jianting Ning,Xiulong Liu,Yulin Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 MA-ACEW，首个在凭证签发中考虑机构权重分布的多机构匿名凭证模型，通过新的 EB-PS 签名原语实现按纪元加权签发与高效跨纪元更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的多机构匿名凭证方案（基于 Shamir 秘密共享或聚合签名）在签发阶段对所有机构一视同仁，忽略了各机构信任度或质押权益的差异；这一问题在权益证明等去中心化系统中尤为突出，因为节点间固有的信任差异无法在凭证签发中被利用。

Method: 提出 MA-ACEW（基于纪元权重的多机构匿名凭证）模型；核心是新的 Epoch-Bound Pointcheval-Sanders 签名（EB-PS）原语，将签名绑定到特定时间纪元，从而支持纪元内按权重签发凭证以及纪元间高效的非交互式凭证更新；形式化 EB-PS 的 EUF-eCMA 不可伪造性要求，并基于新的 STB-GPS 假设证明构造满足该要求；进一步证明整体方案满足不可伪造性、匿名性与盲性；最后给出性能基准测试。

Result: 证明 EB-PS 在新 STB-GPS 假设下满足 EUF-eCMA 不可伪造性；MA-ACEW 构造满足不可伪造性、匿名性和盲性；基准测试表明效率良好，将 128 个部分凭证聚合后的凭证展示平均仅需 10.68 ms。

Conclusion: MA-ACEW 首次将机构权重分布纳入多机构匿名凭证签发，并借助 EB-PS 实现跨纪元的非交互式高效凭证更新，在保证安全性（不可伪造性、匿名性、盲性）的同时具备良好性能，适用于权益证明等具有信任差异的去中心化系统。

Abstract: Anonymous credentials (ACs) are fundamental to privacy-preserving authentication, allowing users to prove possession of attributes without revealing their identities. State-of-the-art ACs distribute credential issuance across multiple authorities, typically employing techniques such as Shamir's secret sharing or aggregate signatures. While this approach enhances system robustness and eliminates a single point of failure, it treats all authorities equally in the credential issuance phase. This uniform treatment disregards the varying levels of trustworthiness or stake held by different authorities. Such a limitation has become particularly problematic in modern decentralized systems like Proof-of-Stake networks, where the inherent trust differentiation among nodes cannot be leveraged in the credential issuance process.
  To address this limitation, we propose the notion of Multi-Authority Anonymous Credentials with Epoch-Based Weights (MA-ACEW), the first Multi-Authority Anonymous Credential (MA-AC) model that considers authorities' weight distribution in credential issuance. Crucially, MA-ACEW enables efficient credential updates when authority weight distributions change across epochs. The core of MA-ACEW is our novel Epoch-Bound Pointcheval-Sanders Signature (EB-PS) primitive, which binds signatures to specific time epochs. This temporal binding enables both weight-based credential issuance within epochs and efficient non-interactive credential updates across epochs. We formalize the EUF-eCMA unforgeability requirement for EB-PS and prove our construction satisfies it under a novel STB-GPS assumption. We then prove that our MA-ACEW construction achieves unforgeability, anonymity, and blindness. Finally, we present benchmarks demonstrating the efficiency of EB-PS and MA-ACEW. Remarkably, presenting a credential aggregated from 128 partial ones takes only 10.68 ms on average.

</details>


### [313] [CASHEWS: Source Preprocessor for LLM-based Malicious Package Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18862)
*Jean-Charles Noirot Ferrand,David Adei,Anders Møller,Alexandros Kapravelos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CASHEWS 是一种 JavaScript 预处理器，通过去混淆、提取打包模块、识别恶意汇点并做反向切片、缩写长字面量与标识符，将源码压缩为紧凑表示，从而提升 LLM 恶意 npm 包检测的覆盖率和准确率并降低分析成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的恶意 npm 包检测依赖对源码的语义理解，在 Shai-Hulud 等供应链攻击中价值显著。但攻击者利用 LLM 上下文窗口有限的弱点，通过代码混淆制造高 token 密度、将恶意代码与良性包捆绑等手段，使检测器跳过大型文件或漏掉恶意行为，形成检测规避面。

Method: CASHEWS 是一个 JavaScript 预处理器：对包源文件进行迭代解码以实现去混淆，提取被打包的模块与动态执行代码，识别恶意汇点并计算到达这些汇点的反向切片，同时对长字面量和标识符进行缩写，最终生成供检测器使用的紧凑表示。

Result: 在 512 个大型包文件、两种扫描器类型和三种 LLM 上，CASHEWS 将分析覆盖率从 69.1–85.7% 提升至 98.8–100%，漏报率最多降低 18.6 个百分点；中位预处理时间为 30 秒，净分析成本降低 34.6%。

Conclusion: 通过在分析前对源码进行预处理，CASHEWS 让研究人员和业界在相同或更低分析成本下使用更强模型进行恶意包检测，推动全注册表范围的 LLM 分析更具可行性。

Abstract: Malicious npm package detection tools now leverage LLMs' semantic understanding of source code to detect malicious intent at scale. This capability has proven invaluable in identifying packages involved in recent supply-chain attacks such as Shai-Hulud. However, threat actors exploit the limited context windows of LLMs through JavaScript techniques such as code obfuscation that yields high token density and bundling malicious code with benign packages, causing detectors to skip large files or miss malicious behavior. This creates an attack surface for evading detection. In this paper, we present CASHEWS, a JavaScript preprocessor that reduces file size by rewriting source code to remove code that is irrelevant to analysis or likely to mislead the model. Given a package source file, CASHEWS deobfuscates it through iterative decoding, extracts bundled modules and dynamically executed code, identifies malicious sinks and computes backward slices that reach them, and abbreviates long literals and identifiers to produce a compact representation for the detector. Across 512 large package files, two scanner types, and three LLMs, CASHEWS increases analysis coverage from 69.1--85.7% to 98.8--100% and reduces the false-negative rate by up to 18.6 percentage points. CASHEWS also has a median preprocessing time of 30 seconds while reducing net analysis cost by 34.6%, making registry-wide LLM-based analysis more practical. By preprocessing source code before analysis, CASHEWS enables researchers and industry practitioners to use more powerful models for malicious package detection at the same or lower analysis cost as less powerful models.

</details>


### [314] [ASLEval: Measuring Privacy Exposure Displacement in LLM Agent Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18864)
*Guosen Wu,Huizhen Huang,Guoxiong Long,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出"隐私暴露位移"概念与ASLEval框架，指出局部评估代理会遗漏多步会话中的真实暴露，并主张以预注册目标、完整可见边界与授权为依据进行评测，同时报告隐私与任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具型LLM智能体的隐私评估常只检查指定动作、最终回复或攻击者报告，这些局部代理会遗漏多步会话中其他位置的未授权暴露，且在不同渠道、报告与工具路径之间缺乏共同的真值基准。

Method: 提出"隐私暴露位移"（局部评估代理与基于目标的会话级暴露之间的不匹配），并设计ASLEval框架：预注册隐藏目标集合、测量所有已声明的可见出口，并保留内部轨迹用于诊断。在多种企业式环境与独立实现的运行时中开展评估。

Result: 观察到三类反复出现的模式：仅看预期出口会漏掉46.9%的暴露（由可见出口并集可恢复）；攻击者自报告既有遗漏又有高误报；符合模式的内​​部证据通常在请求/探测层面先于可见暴露出现。限制模型可见返回会改变该路径，但可能使正常任务成功率消失。独立人工复核支持该判定流程，同时指出更困难的控制台与候选案例。

Conclusion: 呼吁建立新基准：声明完整的可见边界、将结论锚定于预先指定的目标与授权，并把隐私与任务效用一并报告。

Abstract: Privacy evaluations of tool-using LLM agents often inspect a designated action, final response, or attacker report. These local proxies can miss unauthorized exposure elsewhere in a multi-step session and lack common ground truth across outlets, reports, and tool paths. We introduce privacy exposure displacement, the mismatch between a local evaluation proxy and target-grounded session exposure, and ASLEval, an authorization-aware framework that pre-registers a hidden target set, measures all declared visible exits, and reserves internal traces for diagnosis. Across multiple enterprise-style environments and independently implemented runtimes, we observe three recurring patterns. An expected-outlet-only view misses 46.9% of exposure recovered by the visible-exit union; attacker self-reports combine omissions with high false discovery; and schema-aligned internal evidence usually precedes visible exposure at the request/probe level. Reducing model-visible returns changes this path but can eliminate normal-task success. Independent human review supports the adjudication pipeline while identifying harder console and candidate cases. These findings motivate benchmarks that declare the complete visible boundary, ground claims in pre-specified targets and authorization, and report privacy together with task utility.

</details>


### [315] [Hamming Ideals and Grobner Bases for ISD-like Syndrome Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18866)
*Roberto La Scala,Marco Marchesin,Sharwan K. Tiwari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于代数方法求解伴随式译码问题（SDP），通过重新表述汉明重量约束并与信息集译码（ISD）范式结合，给出了GBDecode算法与MultiSolve算法的解码框架，并在Classic McEliece的NIST安全等级1参数下进行实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伴随式译码问题是编码密码学的核心困难问题，传统信息集译码（ISD）方法属于组合搜索范式。作者希望从代数角度重新刻画汉明重量约束，并将代数求解与ISD范式融合，以探索在组合搜索空间与代数求解之间取得可调平衡的可行性。

Method: 1）系统分析汉明簇（Hamming variety），用初等对称函数给出其定义方程；2）利用初等对称函数的卷积恒等式及基于Lucas恒等式的因式分解，引入辅助变量得到次数有界的等价表述；3）在该建模基础上推广ISD范式，提出仅固定信息集子集的ISD-like解码策略，并由GBDecode算法实现；4）采用MultiSolve算法处理代数部分，通过对有限域上若干不定元进行穷举赋值，将单个Grobner基计算替换为多个较简单系统的计算，从而在组合搜索与代数求解间实现可调平衡。

Result: 在随机二元线性码的SDP实例上进行了实验，参数对应Classic McEliece密码系统NIST安全等级1的参数集。实验评估了该组合-代数方法的可行性，并给出了Grobner基技术在ISD-like解码框架中实际表现的观察。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将汉明重量约束的代数刻画与ISD范式结合是可行的，GBDecode与MultiSolve提供了组合搜索与代数求解之间的可调权衡，为Grobner基技术在解码类问题中的应用提供了实践参考。

Abstract: We investigate an algebraic approach to the Syndrome Decoding Problem, based on a reformulation of the Hamming weight constraint and its integration with the Information Set Decoding paradigm. We begin with a systematic analysis of the Hamming variety, deriving its defining equations in terms of elementary symmetric functions. Since these equations may have high degree, we exploit convolution identities for elementary symmetric functions, together with factorizations based on Lucas' identity, to derive an equivalent formulation with auxiliary variables and equations of bounded degree.
  Building on this modeling, we generalize the ISD paradigm through an ISD-like decoding strategy, implemented by the GBDecode algorithm, in which only a subset of an information set is fixed. This approach reduces the size of the combinatorial search space at the cost of solving the associated multivariate nonlinear systems. To handle this algebraic component, we employ the MultiSolve algorithm, which replaces a single Grobner basis computation with a collection of computations on simpler systems, obtained by exhaustively assigning a varying number of indeterminates over the finite field. This provides a tunable balance between combinatorial search and algebraic solving.
  We evaluate the resulting approach experimentally on instances of the Syndrome Decoding Problem for random binary linear codes, using parameters corresponding to the NIST Security Category 1 parameter set of the Classic McEliece cryptosystem. The experiments assess the feasibility of this combinatorial-algebraic approach and provide insights into the practical behavior of Grobner basis techniques within an ISD-like decoding framework.

</details>


### [316] [BadQubits: An LLM-Based Framework for Static Pre-Execution Detection of Structurally Harmful Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18965)
*Justin Woodring,Lamine Noureddine,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 BadQubits，一个基于 LLM 的 OpenQASM 2.0 电路静态预执行有害性检测框架，通过微调 Qwen Coder 2.5 7B 实现高精度检测，并在混淆去除与对抗扰动下证明其优于直方图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理执行层的量子电路威胁需要在运行前检测，因为运行时动态检查受限于测量不可逆性与量子态经典模拟的指数成本。现有方法难以在预执行阶段有效识别结构性有害电路。

Method: 构建包含 1,500 个电路的数据集（1,000 个来自 MQTBench 的良性程序与 500 个源自三类已记录物理层威胁原语的合成攻击电路），评估四种代码理解 LLM 架构；采用受限 LoRA 微调，并与基于门的词袋 CNN 基线在渐进式混淆去除和对抗句法扰动下对比；通过相关性分析考察模型决策依据的特征。

Result: 微调后的 Qwen Coder 2.5 7B 达到 92.67% 分类准确率与 96.1% 有害电路召回率；四个基础模型中有两个在受限 LoRA 微调下无法泛化。CNN 的有害召回率从 100% 降至 17%，而微调 LLM 仅从 96.1% 降至 91.2%。相关性分析显示模型决策追踪 SWAP 密度与测量时序等威胁定义特征，而非寄存器命名等生成器特有伪迹。

Conclusion: 架构感知的模型选择是必要设计考量而非次要调参；LLM 保留的 token 级序列结构使其在混淆去除与句法扰动下表现更稳健；BadQubits 可作为物理执行层威胁的预执行静态检测方案。

Abstract: This paper presents BadQubits, an LLM-based framework for static pre-execution detection of structurally harmful OpenQASM 2.0 circuits. The framework targets physical-execution-layer threats by analyzing submitted circuits prior to runtime, where dynamic inspection is constrained by measurement irreversibility and the exponential cost of classical quantum-state simulation. We evaluate four code-understanding LLM architectures on a dataset of 1,500 circuits consisting of 1,000 benign programs from MQTBench[33] and 500 synthetic attack circuits derived from three documented physical-layer threat primitives. Our fine-tuned Qwen Coder 2.5 7B model achieves 92.67% classification accuracy and 96.1% harmful-circuit recall. Two of the four evaluated base models fail to generalize under constrained LoRA fine-tuning, indicating that architecture-aware model selection is a necessary design consideration rather than a minor tuning choice. To characterize what the detector has learned, we compare it against a bag-of-gates CNN under progressive confound removal and adversarial syntactic perturbation. The CNN's harmful-circuit recall drops from 100% to 17%, while the fine-tuned LLM decreases only from 96.1% to 91.2%. We attribute this gap to the sequential structure retained in token-level LLM inputs but discarded by histogram-based baselines. A correlation analysis further shows that model decisions track threat-defining features, specifically SWAP density and measurement timing, rather than generator-specific artifacts such as register naming.

</details>


### [317] [Context-Aware Operational Security for Autonomous Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19021)
*Burak Tufekci,Cihan Tunc*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DUDE-IDS，利用LSTM网络对无人机传感器数据与操作命令进行时序异常检测，直接部署于机载任务计算机，以98%准确率识别GPS欺骗、MITM、重放与DoS攻击，并验证了实时性与资源功耗适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主无人机服务在多个领域受到关注，但系统失效或网络攻击会造成严重经济损失与安全隐患。传统安全措施因无人机的计算资源与电量受限，以及其移动性带来的时序与顺序行为特征而难以适用，因此需要一种轻量且能捕捉时序特征的机载安全方案。

Method: 采用循环神经网络，特别是LSTM网络，利用其时序与序列分析能力，对自主无人机的传感器数据与操作命令进行异常检测，形成DUDE-IDS（Denial of Usage Detection Engine IDS）。该方案被集成到无人机任务计算机上，直接运行于无人机而非边缘节点或地面控制站，实时监控数据流并检测潜在威胁。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法可有效识别与GPS欺骗、中间人攻击（MITM）、重放攻击和拒绝服务攻击（DoS）相关的异常，准确率达98%。同时在不同配置下评估了资源占用与功耗，证明其在无人机实时作业中的适用性。

Conclusion: 基于LSTM的机载异常检测方案能够在资源受限的无人机平台上实现高精度、实时的安全监控，为自主无人机服务的安全可靠部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous drone-based services have been gaining significant interest across various application domains due to their mobility, flexibility, cost-effectiveness, and capability to integrate various sensors and actuators. However, operational failures or cyberattacks targeting drone systems can lead to severe economic impacts and safety concerns. Hence, ensuring secure and reliable autonomous drone operations is essential for the safe deployment of drone-enabled services. Nevertheless, the traditional security measures fall short due to drones' limited computational resources and power budget (battery), as well as the temporal and sequential behavior of drone operations due to drones' mobile nature. In this paper, we address this gap by utilizing Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs), specifically focusing on Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, for autonomous drone operation security and reliability due to their temporal and sequential analysis capabilities. We leverage these capabilities for anomaly detection in autonomous drone sensor data and operation commands, which we refer to as Denial of Usage Detection Engine IDS (DUDE-IDS). We integrated the proposed DUDE-IDS into the drone mission computer (i.e., operating directly on drones rather than an edge node or ground control stations) to monitor data flows and to detect potential threats in real-time.
